EXECUTIVE READING

先给结论，再进入源码

核心机制

它不是在旧 Python 循环上继续打补丁，而是把一套 Codex 级 Rust Harness 换了产品入口，再加入自己的多 Harness 能力。

证据规模

26 条 finding / 26 处引用

主要边界

当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

适用建设

它不是在旧 Python 循环上继续打补丁，而是把一套 Codex 级 Rust Harness 换了产品入口，再加入自己的多 Harness 能力。

值得借鉴

  • 它不是在旧 Python 循环上继续打补丁，而是把一套 Codex 级 Rust Harness 换了产品入口，再加入自己的多 Harness 能力。
  • “还能被 Codex SDK 当成 Codex 用”是 CI 级目标，不是偶然能跑。
  • 同一个底盘能换不同“驾驶舱”：改变系统提示词、消息格式、工具名称与参数，去适配最合适的模型习惯。

需要警惕

  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

阅读方式

  • 先看固定提交，再看调用链
  • 事实、推断、风险分栏
  • 每条 finding 可回到源码
00 · METHOD

研究口径：不是 README 摘要

README POLICY

README 只定位入口和产品命名；实现结论必须回到运行时代码、契约或测试。

FACT POLICY

L1 运行代码优先，L2 类型/事件契约补足，L3/L4 交叉验证，L5 推断单独标记。

架构、时序、能力、主循环和指令执行图都独占一段；节点来自源码 finding 与指令线索，点击节点可查看固定提交、行号和执行语义。

02 · COVERAGE MAP

覆盖维度与证据等级

恢复、测试与取舍verifiedL1 / L3

当前快照是 Rust/Codex 分叉式产品，保留 Codex SDK/协议兼容并通过包变体重品牌。

架构与所有权verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

turn/step 状态机、step 快照、pending input、自动压缩与 stop hook。

架构与所有权verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

Responses/Chat/Messages、provider 默认 harness、请求/响应整形与兼容边界。

模型与上下文verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

规范化历史、工具配对、多模态裁剪、Harness-aware 压缩。

工具注册与执行verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

可见 spec 与 registry 分离、代码模式、Harness aliases、文件变更事件。

安全、审批与权限verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

审批与权限 profile、路径双重校验、macOS/Linux/Windows 原生隔离。

连接器、插件与 MCPverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

MCP catalog、Skills/Plugins/Extensions、完整 hooks 生命周期。

协作与子 AgentverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

线程树控制面、fork/message/resume、深度与并发限制；Harness aliases 可复用同一子 Agent 系统。

观测、日志与回放verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

JSONL rollout、SQLite 镜像、文件变更 lifecycle、Open Interpreter 独立 analytics endpoint 与 opt-out。

  259  pub fn request_permissions_tool_description() -> String {
  260      "Request additional filesystem or network permissions from the user and wait for the client to grant a subset of the requested permission profile. Use environment_id to target a specific attached environment; omit it to use the primary environment. Relative filesystem paths resolve against the selected environment cwd.   264  fn unified_exec_output_schema() -> Value {
  265      json!({
  266          "type": "object",
  267          "properties": {
  268              "chunk_id": {
  269                  "type": "string",
  270                  "description": "Chunk identifier included when the response reports one."
  271              },
  272              "wall_time_seconds": {
  273                  "type": "number",
  274                  "description": "Elapsed wall time spent waiting for output in seconds."
  275              },
  276              "exit_code": {
  277                  "type": "number",
  278                  "description": "Process exit code when the command finished during this call."
  279              },
  280              "session_id": {
  281                  "type": "number",
  282                  "description": "Session identifier to pass to write_stdin when the process is still running."
  283              },
  284              "original_token_count": {
  285                  "type": "number",
  286                  "description": "Approximate token count before output truncation."
  287              },
  288              "output": {
  289                  "type": "string",
  290                  "description": "Command output text, possibly truncated."
  291              }
  292          },
  293          "required": ["wall_time_seconds", "output"],
  294          "additionalProperties": false
  295      })
  296  }
  297
  298  fn create_approval_parameters(
  299      exec_permission_approvals_enabled: bool,
  300  ) -> BTreeMap<String, JsonSchema> {
  301      let mut sandbox_permission_values = vec![json!("use_default")];
  302      if exec_permission_approvals_enabled {
  303          sandbox_permission_values.push(json!("with_additional_permissions"));

  102  pub fn create_spawn_agent_tool_v2(options: SpawnAgentToolOptions) -> ToolSpec {
  103      let available_models_description = options.expose_spawn_agent_model_overrides.then(|| {
  104          spawn_agent_models_description(&options.available_models, options.multi_agent_version)
  105      });
  106      let inherited_model_guidance = (options.expose_spawn_agent_model_overrides
  107          && !options.hide_agent_type_model_reasoning)
  108          .then_some(SPAWN_AGENT_INHERITED_MODEL_GUIDANCE);
  109      let mut properties = spawn_agent_common_properties_v2(&options.agent_type_description);
  110      if !options.expose_agent_type {
  111          properties.remove("agent_type");
  112      }
  113      if options.hide_agent_type_model_reasoning {
  114          properties.remove("service_tier");
  115      }
  116      if !options.expose_spawn_agent_model_overrides {
  117          properties.remove("model");
  118          properties.remove("reasoning_effort");
  119      }
  120      properties.insert(
  121          "task_name".to_string(),
  122          JsonSchema::string(Some(
  123              "Task name for the new agent. Use lowercase letters, digits, and underscores."
  124                  .to_string(),
  125          )),
  126      );
  127
  128      ToolSpec::Function(ResponsesApiTool {
  129          name: "spawn_agent".to_string(),
  130          description: spawn_agent_tool_description_v2(
  131              available_models_description.as_deref(),
  132              inherited_model_guidance,
  133              options.usage_hint_text,
  134          ),
  135          strict: false,
  136          defer_loading: None,
  137          parameters: JsonSchema::object(
  138              properties,
  139              Some(vec!["task_name".to_string(), "message".to_string()]),
  140              Some(false.into()),
  141          ),
  142          output_schema: Some(spawn_agent_output_schema_v2(
  143              options.hide_agent_type_model_reasoning,
  144          )),
  145      })
  146  }

  159          (
  160              "command".to_string(),
  161              JsonSchema::string(Some(
  162                  "Shell script to run in the user's default shell.".to_string(),
  163              )),
  164          ),
  165          (
  166              "workdir".to_string(),
  167              JsonSchema::string(Some(
  168                  "Working directory for the command. Defaults to the turn cwd.".to_string(),
  169              )),
  170          ),
  171          (
  172              "timeout_ms".to_string(),
  173              JsonSchema::number(Some(
  174                  "Maximum command runtime. Defaults to 10000 ms.".to_string(),
  175              )),
  176          ),
  177      ]);
  178      if options.allow_login_shell {
  179          properties.insert(
  180              "login".to_string(),
  181              JsonSchema::boolean(Some(
  182                  "True runs with login shell semantics; false disables them. Defaults to true."
  183                      .to_string(),
  184              )),
  185          );
  186      }
  187      properties.extend(create_approval_parameters(
  188          options.exec_permission_approvals_enabled,
  189      ));
  190
  191      let description = if cfg!(windows) {
  192          format!(
  193              r#"Runs a Powershell command (Windows) and returns its output.
  194
  195  Examples of valid command strings:
  196
  197  - ls -a (show hidden): "Get-ChildItem -Force"
  198  - recursive find by name: "Get-ChildItem -Recurse -Filter *.py"
  199  - recursive grep: "Get-ChildItem -Path C:\\myrepo -Recurse | Select-String -Pattern 'TODO' -CaseSensitive"
  200  - ps aux | grep python: "Get-Process | Where-Object {{ $_.ProcessName -like '*python*' }}"
  201  - setting an env var: "$env:FOO='bar'; echo $env:FOO"

  908
  909  impl FeatureToggles {
  910      fn to_overrides(&self) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<String>> {
  911          let mut v = Vec::new();
  912          for feature in &self.enable {
  913              Self::validate_feature(feature)?;
  914              v.push(format!("features.{feature}=true"));
  915          }
  916          for feature in &self.disable {
  917              Self::validate_feature(feature)?;
  918              v.push(format!("features.{feature}=false"));
  919          }
  920          Ok(v)
  921      }
  922
  923      fn validate_feature(feature: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
  924          if is_known_feature_key(feature) {
  925              Ok(())
  926          } else {
  927              anyhow::bail!("Unknown feature flag: {feature}")
  928          }
  929      }
  930  }
  931

  936  }

  946  }

cmd

CLI 子命令 · Rust command constructor · codex-rs/cli/src/main.rs:782
源码 ↗

On Windows, run via cmd.exe so .CMD/.BAT are correctly resolved (PATHEXT semantics).

cmd→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  777                  // PowerShell metacharacters like `|` before PowerShell sees
  778                  // the installer command.
  779                  std::process::Command::new(cmd).args(args).status()?
  780              } else {
  781                  // On Windows, run via cmd.exe so .CMD/.BAT are correctly resolved (PATHEXT semantics).
  782                  std::process::Command::new("cmd")
  783                      .args(["/C", &cmd_str])
  784                      .status()?
  785              }
  786          }
  787          #[cfg(not(windows))]
  788          {
  789              let (cmd, args) = action.command_args();
  790              let command_path = crate::wsl_paths::normalize_for_wsl(cmd);
  791              let normalized_args: Vec<String> = args
  792                  .iter()
  793                  .map(crate::wsl_paths::normalize_for_wsl)
  794                  .collect();
  795              std::process::Command::new(&command_path)
  796                  .args(&normalized_args)
  797                  .status()?
  798          }
  799      };
  800      if !status.success() {
  801          anyhow::bail!("`{cmd_str}` failed with status {status}");
  802      }
  803      println!("\n🎉 Update ran successfully! Please restart Codex.");
  804      Ok(())
  805  }
  806
  807  fn run_update_command() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
  808      #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
  809      {
  810          anyhow::bail!(
  811              "`codex update` is not available in debug builds. Install a release build of Codex to use this command."
  812          );
  813      }
  814
  815      #[cfg(not(debug_assertions))]
  816      {
  817          let Some(action) = codex_tui::get_update_action() else {
  818              anyhow::bail!(
  819                  "Could not detect the Codex installation method. Please update manually: https://developers.openai.com/codex/cli/"
  820              );
  821          };
  822          run_update_action(action)
  823      }
  824  }

  911          let mut v = Vec::new();
  912          for feature in &self.enable {
  913              Self::validate_feature(feature)?;
  914              v.push(format!("features.{feature}=true"));
  915          }
  916          for feature in &self.disable {
  917              Self::validate_feature(feature)?;
  918              v.push(format!("features.{feature}=false"));
  919          }
  920          Ok(v)
  921      }
  922
  923      fn validate_feature(feature: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
  924          if is_known_feature_key(feature) {
  925              Ok(())
  926          } else {
  927              anyhow::bail!("Unknown feature flag: {feature}")
  928          }
  929      }
  930  }

pair

CLI 子命令 · clap Subcommand variant · codex-rs/cli/src/remote_control_cmd.rs:61
源码 ↗

/ Create and print a short-lived manual pairing code.

pair→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   56
   57      /// Stop the app-server daemon.
   58      Stop,
   59
   60      /// Create and print a short-lived manual pairing code.
   61      Pair,
   62  }
   63
   64  pub(crate) async fn run(
   65      command: RemoteControlCommand,
   66      arg0_paths: Arg0DispatchPaths,
   67      root_config_overrides: CliConfigOverrides,
   68  ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
   69      match command.subcommand {
   70          None => {
   71              print_remote_control_progress(
   72                  command.json,
   73                  "Starting app-server with remote control enabled...",
   74              )?;
   75              run_foreground_remote_control(command.json, arg0_paths, root_config_overrides).await?;
   76          }
   77          Some(RemoteControlSubcommand::Start) => {
   78              print_remote_control_progress(
   79                  command.json,
   80                  "Starting app-server daemon with remote control enabled...",
   81              )?;
   82              let output = codex_app_server_daemon::ensure_remote_control_ready().await?;
   83              print_remote_control_start_output(&output, command.json)?;
   84          }
   85          Some(RemoteControlSubcommand::Stop) => {
   86              print_remote_control_progress(command.json, "Stopping remote control...")?;
   87              let output = codex_app_server_daemon::run(AppServerLifecycleCommand::Stop).await?;
   88              print_remote_control_stop_output(&output, command.json)?;
   89          }
   90          Some(RemoteControlSubcommand::Pair) => {
   91              let output = codex_app_server_daemon::start_remote_control_pairing().await?;
   92              print_remote_control_pairing_output(&output, command.json)?;
   93          }
   94      }
   95      Ok(())
   96  }
   97
   98  fn print_remote_control_progress(json: bool, message: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
   99      if json {
  100          return Ok(());
  101      }
  102
  103      println!("{message}");
  104      std::io::stdout()
  105          .flush()
  106          .context("failed to flush remote-control progress message")?;
  107      Ok(())
  108  }
  109
  110  async fn run_foreground_remote_control(
  111      json: bool,
  112      arg0_paths: Arg0DispatchPaths,
  113      root_config_overrides: CliConfigOverrides,
  114  ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
  115      let socket_dir = tempfile::Builder::new()
  116          .prefix("codex-rc-")
  117          .tempdir_in("/tmp")
  118          .or_else(|_| tempfile::tempdir())
  119          .context("failed to create private app-server socket directory")?;
  120      let socket_path = socket_dir.path().join("rc.sock");

  707      AbsolutePathBuf::relative_to_current_dir(raw)
  708          .map_err(|err| format!("failed to resolve socket path `{raw}`: {err}"))
  709  }
  710
  711  fn format_exit_messages(exit_info: AppExitInfo, color_enabled: bool) -> Vec<String> {
  712      let is_fatal = matches!(&exit_info.exit_reason, ExitReason::Fatal(_));
  713      let AppExitInfo {
  714          token_usage,
  715          thread_id: conversation_id,
  716          resume_hint,
  717          ..
  718      } = exit_info;
  719
  720      let mut lines = Vec::new();
  721      if !token_usage.is_zero() {
  722          lines.push(token_usage.to_string());
  723      }
  724
  725      if let Some(resume_cmd) = resume_hint {
  726          let command = if color_enabled {
  727              resume_cmd.cyan().to_string()
  728          } else {
  729              resume_cmd
  730          };
  731          lines.push(format!("To continue this session, run {command}"));
  732      } else if is_fatal && let Some(conversation_id) = conversation_id {

/agent

交互 slash 指令 · SlashCommand enum variant · codex-rs/tui/src/slash_command.rs:44
源码 ↗

交互层可直接触发的 slash 指令；本卡展示它的注册点和进入处理器后的执行边界。

/agent→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   39      App,
   40      Init,
   41      Compact,
   42      Plan,

   44      Agent,
   45      Side,
   46      Btw,
   47      Copy,
   48      Raw,
   49      Diff,
   50      Mention,
   51      Status,
   52      Usage,

   55      Statusline,

   57      #[strum(to_string = "pets", serialize = "pet")]
   58      Pets,

   60      Apps,

   62      Logout,
   63      Quit,
   64      Exit,
   65      Feedback,

   67      Ps,
   68      #[strum(to_string = "stop", serialize = "clean")]
   69      Stop,
   70      Clear,
   71      Personality,
   72      TestApproval,
   73      #[strum(serialize = "subagents")]
   74      MultiAgents,
   75      // Debugging commands.
   76      #[strum(serialize = "debug-m-drop")]
   77      MemoryDrop,
   78      #[strum(serialize = "debug-m-update")]
   79      MemoryUpdate,
   80  }
   81
   82  impl SlashCommand {
   83      /// User-visible description shown in the popup.
   84      pub fn description(self) -> &'static str {
   85          match self {
   86              SlashCommand::Feedback => "send logs to maintainers",
   87              SlashCommand::New => "start a new chat during a conversation",
   88              SlashCommand::Init => match codex_product_info::Product::current() {
   89                  codex_product_info::Product::Codex => {
   90                      "create an AGENTS.md file with instructions for Codex"
   91                  }
   92                  codex_product_info::Product::OpenInterpreter => {

   94                  }

   99              SlashCommand::Resume => "resume a saved chat",

01
DIMENSION · ARCHITECTURE

架构与所有权

7 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 核心差异是一层多 Harness 仿真目录

源码事实

源码内有 Claude Code/full+bare、DeepSeek TUI、Kimi CLI、Kimi Code、Little Coder、mini-swe-agent、OpenCode、Pi、Qwen Code、SWE-agent、Terminus 2、ZCode 与 Minimal 等独立请求构造器；app-server 将主要可选项按 wire API 编成 catalog。

白话解释

同一个底盘能换不同“驾驶舱”：改变系统提示词、消息格式、工具名称与参数，去适配最合适的模型习惯。

对自研 Harness 的含义

这比普通 provider adapter 更深，但也不是把这些项目的完整运行时都嵌入进来。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–8
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 9–18
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

边界 / 风险
  • catalog 的可选项与底层 enum 并非一一相等，例如 ZCode 存在于底层实现但未列入这一段 UI catalog。

02
L2factarchitecture

02 · Provider、Wire API、Harness 是三层独立选择

源码事实

app-server DTO 分开暴露 Provider、Model、Harness；Provider 包含 base URL/env key/wire API，Harness 是可推荐的独立选项。默认推断按 provider/model/base URL 把 Claude→claude-code、Kimi→kimi-code、Qwen→qwen-code、DeepSeek→claude-code-bare。

白话解释

“连哪家服务器”“用哪个模型”“让请求长得像哪个 Coding Agent”不是同一个开关。

对自研 Harness 的含义

可做跨模型的 Harness A/B；默认映射是产品经验规则，不代表所有组合都兼容。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 8–25
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 26–44
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L3factarchitecture

03 · 路由矩阵在代码里硬校验，不兼容组合直接报错

源码事实

Responses 原生走 Responses；Chat 非 Claude Harness 走 chat compatibility；Claude full/bare 可对 Responses/Chat 做 profile shaping；Messages 只允许 Claude 与 ZCode 原生路径，其他 Harness 和 Native Messages 返回 InvalidRequest。单元测试覆盖这些分支。

白话解释

不是把任意协议和任意外壳随便相乘；路由表会在发请求前拦住不支持的组合。

对自研 Harness 的含义

错误更早、更可诊断；catalog 的 Native/ALL 表述仍需与 routing 的 Native+Messages 拒绝一起理解。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 5–77
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 78–151
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

边界 / 风险
  • app-server catalog 把 Native 标成 ALL_WIRE_APIS，但底层 routing 对 Native+Messages 明确报错；这是当前提交可见的接口不一致。

   50  impl StreamTransportRoute {
   51      pub(crate) fn supports_responses_websocket(self) -> bool {
   52          matches!(self, Self::ResponsesApi)
   53      }
   54  }

   56  pub(crate) fn resolve_stream_transport_route(
   57      wire_api: WireApi,
   58      harness: &Harness,
   59  ) -> Result<StreamTransportRoute, CodexErr> {
   60      match (wire_api, harness) {
   61          (WireApi::Responses, Harness::ClaudeCode | Harness::ClaudeCodeBare) => Ok(
   62              StreamTransportRoute::ClaudeCodeResponses(claude_code_profile_route(harness)),
   63          ),
   64          (WireApi::Responses, _) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ResponsesApi),
   65          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::ClaudeCode | Harness::ClaudeCodeBare) => Ok(
   66              StreamTransportRoute::ClaudeCodeChat(claude_code_profile_route(harness)),
   67          ),
   68          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::KimiCli) => {
   69              Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(ChatHarnessRoute::KimiCli))
   70          }
   71          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::KimiCode) => {
   72              Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(ChatHarnessRoute::KimiCode))
   73          }
   74          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::LittleCoder) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(
   75              ChatHarnessRoute::LittleCoder,
   76          )),
   77          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::MiniSweAgent) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(
   78              ChatHarnessRoute::MiniSweAgent,
   79          )),
   80          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::OpenCode) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(
   81              ChatHarnessRoute::OpenCode,
   82          )),
   83          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::Pi) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(ChatHarnessRoute::Pi)),
   84          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::DeepSeekTui) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(
   85              ChatHarnessRoute::DeepSeekTui,
   86          )),
   87          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::Minimal) => {
   88              Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(ChatHarnessRoute::Minimal))
   89          }
   90          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::QwenCode) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(
   91              ChatHarnessRoute::QwenCode,
   92          )),
   93          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::SweAgent) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(
   94              ChatHarnessRoute::SweAgent,
   95          )),
   96          (WireApi::Chat, Harness::Terminus2) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatHarness(
   97              ChatHarnessRoute::Terminus2,
   98          )),
   99          (WireApi::Chat, _) => Ok(StreamTransportRoute::ChatCompletionsCompat),
  100          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::ClaudeCode | Harness::ClaudeCodeBare) => {
  101              Ok(StreamTransportRoute::MessagesHarness(
  102                  MessagesHarnessRoute::ClaudeCode,
  103              ))
  104          }
  105          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::ZCode) => {
  106              Ok(StreamTransportRoute::MessagesHarness(MessagesHarnessRoute::ZCode))
  107          }
  108          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::KimiCli) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  109              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"kimi-cli\"".to_string(),
  110          )),
  111          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::KimiCode) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  112              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"kimi-code\"".to_string(),
  113          )),
  114          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::LittleCoder) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  115              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"little-coder\"".to_string(),
  116          )),
  117          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::MiniSweAgent) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  118              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"mini-swe-agent\""
  119                  .to_string(),
  120          )),
  121          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::OpenCode) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  122              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"opencode\"".to_string(),
  123          )),
  124          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::Pi) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  125              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"pi\"".to_string(),
  126          )),
  127          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::DeepSeekTui) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  128              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"deepseek-tui\"".to_string(),
  129          )),
  130          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::Minimal) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  131              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"minimal\"".to_string(),
  132          )),
  133          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::QwenCode) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  134              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"qwen-code\"".to_string(),
  135          )),
  136          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::SweAgent) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  137              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"swe-agent\"".to_string(),
  138          )),
  139          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::Terminus2) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  140              "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"terminus-2\"".to_string(),
  141          )),
  142          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::Native) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  143              "wire_api = \"messages\" requires a harness-native transport; configure harness = \"claude-code\" or \"claude-code-bare\" for Anthropic-style sessions"
  144                  .to_string(),
  145          )),
  146          (WireApi::Messages, Harness::Other(harness_name)) => Err(CodexErr::InvalidRequest(
  147              format!(
  148                  "wire_api = \"messages\" is not supported by harness = \"{harness_name}\""
  149              ),
  150          )),
  151      }
  152  }

  154  /// Maps the claude-code harness flavor onto the shaping profile used by the
  155  /// non-Messages wires. Callers only reach this for `ClaudeCode`/`ClaudeCodeBare`.
  156  fn claude_code_profile_route(harness: &Harness) -> ClaudeCodeProfileRoute {
  157      match harness {
  158          Harness::ClaudeCodeBare => ClaudeCodeProfileRoute::Bare,
  159          _ => ClaudeCodeProfileRoute::Full,
  160      }
  161  }

  165      match harness {
  166          Harness::ClaudeCode => "claude-code",
  167          Harness::ClaudeCodeBare => "claude-code-bare",
  168          Harness::Native => "",
  169          Harness::DeepSeekTui => "deepseek-tui",

  172          Harness::ZCode => "zcode",
  173          Harness::LittleCoder => "little-coder",
  174          Harness::MiniSweAgent => "mini-swe-agent",
  175          Harness::OpenCode => "opencode",
  176          Harness::Pi => "pi",
  177          Harness::Minimal => "minimal",
  178          Harness::QwenCode => "qwen-code",
  179          Harness::SweAgent => "swe-agent",
  180          Harness::Terminus2 => "terminus-2",
  181          Harness::Other(name) => name.as_str(),
  182      }

04
L1factarchitecture

04 · 请求仿真的单一入口统一处理前置与后置整形

源码事实

request.rs 明确声明为 model client 与 chat harness emulation 的单一集成点；它按 route 调各 builder，判断 YOLO、可加 Harness guidance、为 OpenCode 生成独立 title request，并为 mini-SWE/SWE-agent/Terminus 2 注入响应流后处理。

白话解释

每种外壳的怪癖被收在一个总路由器里：发出前改请求，回来后必要时把文本动作翻译成工具动作。

对自研 Harness 的含义

新增 Harness 的改动面较集中；后处理型工具提取比原生 function call 更脆弱，必须依赖格式测试。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–3
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 4–8
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   59      pub(crate) postprocess: ChatHarnessPostprocess,
   60  }

   71          pending_completion: bool,

L1factarchitecture

05 · 各 Harness 不只换 prompt，还重写历史和工具协议

源码事实

例如 Pi builder 生成自己的 system prompt/tools/thinking 配置，把 ResponseItem 历史改成 Chat messages，跳过 contextual user message、配对工具调用与结果、保留 reasoning_content，并把 LocalShell/Custom tool call 映射成 Pi 认识的名字；Little Coder 与 OpenCode 各自拥有不同 system prompt、tool kinds 和 title/search-agent 逻辑。

白话解释

这不是贴一张角色卡，而是把整段对话和工具说明书翻译成目标 Agent 的“母语”。

对自研 Harness 的含义

模型行为更接近目标 Harness；翻译层必须维护 call/result 配对和隐藏上下文，升级成本高。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 21–69
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 70–120
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   53  fn build_system_prompt(prompt: &Prompt) -> String {
   54      let cwd = prompt.cwd.as_deref().unwrap_or_else(|| Path::new("."));
   55      let date = chrono::Local::now().format("%Y-%m-%d");
   56      let source_dir = std::env::var("OPEN_INTERPRETER_PI_SOURCE_DIR")
   57          .ok()
   58          .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
   59          .or_else(|| {
   60              std::env::var("PI_SOURCE_DIR")
   61                  .ok()
   62                  .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
   63          })
   64          .unwrap_or_else(|| "<pi>".to_string());
   65      format!(
   66          "You are an expert coding assistant operating inside pi, a coding agent harness. You help users by reading files, executing commands, editing code, and writing new files.\n\nAvailable tools:\n- read: Read file contents\n- bash: Execute bash commands (ls, grep, find, etc.)\n- edit: Make precise file edits with exact text replacement, including multiple disjoint edits in one call\n- write: Create or overwrite files\n\nIn addition to the tools above, you may have access to other custom tools depending on the project.\n\nGuidelines:\n- Use bash for file operations like ls, rg, find\n- Use read to examine files instead of cat or sed.\n- Use edit for precise changes (edits[].oldText must match exactly)\n- When changing multiple separate locations in one file, use one edit call with multiple entries in edits[] instead of multiple edit calls\n- Each edits[].oldText is matched against the original file, not after earlier edits are applied. Do not emit overlapping or nested edits. Merge nearby changes into one edit.\n- Keep edits[].oldText as small as possible while still being unique in the file. Do not pad with large unchanged regions.\n- Use write only for new files or complete rewrites.\n- Be concise in your responses\n- Show file paths clearly when working with files\n\nPi documentation (read only when the user asks about pi itself, its SDK, extensions, themes, skills, or TUI):\n- Main documentation: <pi>/packages/coding-agent/README.md\n- Additional docs: <pi>/packages/coding-agent/docs\n- Examples: <pi>/packages/coding-agent/examples (extensions, custom tools, SDK)\n- When reading pi docs or examples, resolve docs/... under Additional docs and examples/... under Examples, not the current working directory\n- When asked about: extensions (docs/extensions.md, examples/extensions/), themes (docs/themes.md), skills (docs/skills.md), prompt templates (docs/prompt-templates.md), TUI components (docs/tui.md), keybindings (docs/keybindings.md), SDK integrations (docs/sdk.md), custom providers (docs/custom-provider.md), adding models (docs/models.md), pi packages (docs/packages.md)\n- When working on pi topics, read the docs and examples, and follow .md cross-references before implementing\n- Always read pi .md files completely and follow links to related docs (e.g., tui.md for TUI API details)\nCurrent date: {date}\nCurrent working directory: {cwd}",
   67          cwd = cwd.display()
   68      )
   69      .replace("<pi>", &source_dir)
   70  }

   72  pub(crate) fn build_messages(items: &[ResponseItem]) -> Result<Vec<Value>, serde_json::Error> {
   73      let mut messages = Vec::new();
   74      let mut pending_tool_calls = Vec::new();
   75      let mut awaiting_tool_call_ids = Vec::new();
   76      let mut pending_assistant_content: Option<String> = None;
   77      let mut pending_reasoning_content: Option<String> = None;
   78
   79      for item in items {
   80          match item {
   81              ResponseItem::Message { role, content, .. } => match role.as_str() {
   82                  "assistant" => {
   83                      if let Some(message_content) = message_content(content)
   84                          && !message_content.is_empty()
   85                      {
   86                          pending_assistant_content = Some(message_content);
   87                      }
   88                  }
   89                  "user" => {
   90                      if is_contextual_user_message_content(content) {
   91                          continue;
   92                      }
   93                      discard_unanswered_tool_calls(
   94                          &mut pending_tool_calls,
   95                          &mut awaiting_tool_call_ids,
   96                          &mut pending_assistant_content,
   97                          &mut pending_reasoning_content,
   98                      );
   99                      flush_pending_assistant_message(
  100                          &mut messages,
  101                          &mut pending_assistant_content,
  102                          &mut pending_reasoning_content,
  103                      );
  104                      if let Some(message_content) = message_content(content) {
  105                          messages.push(json!({
  106                              "role": "user",
  107                              "content": [
  108                                  {
  109                                      "type": "text",
  110                                      "text": message_content,
  111                                  }
  112                              ],
  113                          }));
  114                      }
  115                  }
  116                  "developer" => {}
  117                  _ => {}
  118              },
  119              ResponseItem::FunctionCall {
  120                  name,
  121                  arguments,
  122                  call_id,
  123                  ..
  124              } => {
  125                  pending_tool_calls.push(json!({
  126                      "id": call_id,
  127                      "type": "function",
  128                      "function": {
  129                          "name": name,
  130                          "arguments": compact_json_arguments(name, arguments),
  131                      }
  132                  }));
  133              }
  134              ResponseItem::LocalShellCall {
  135                  id,
  136                  call_id,
  137                  action,
  138                  ..
  139              } => {
  140                  let call_id = call_id
  141                      .clone()
  142                      .or_else(|| id.as_ref().map(ToString::to_string))
  143                      .ok_or_else(|| {
  144                          serde_json::Error::io(std::io::Error::new(
  145                              std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
  146                              "local_shell history item missing call id",
  147                          ))
  148                      })?;
  149                  let arguments = match action {
  150                      LocalShellAction::Exec(exec) => json!({
  151                          "command": exec.command,
  152                          "timeout": exec.timeout_ms.map(|timeout| timeout / 1000),
  153                      })
  154                      .to_string(),
  155                  };
  156                  pending_tool_calls.push(json!({
  157                      "id": call_id,
  158                      "type": "function",
  159                      "function": {
  160                          "name": "bash",
  161                          "arguments": compact_json_arguments("bash", &arguments),
  162                      }
  163                  }));
  164              }
  165              ResponseItem::FunctionCallOutput {
  166                  call_id, output, ..
  167              }
  168              | ResponseItem::CustomToolCallOutput {
  169                  call_id, output, ..
  170              } => {
  171                  flush_pending_tool_calls(
  172                      &mut messages,
  173                      &mut pending_tool_calls,
  174                      &mut awaiting_tool_call_ids,
  175                      &mut pending_assistant_content,
  176                      &mut pending_reasoning_content,
  177                  );
  178                  if awaiting_tool_call_ids.iter().any(|id| id == call_id) {
  179                      messages.push(json!({
  180                          "role": "tool",
  181                          "content": tool_output_content(output),
  182                          "tool_call_id": call_id,
  183                      }));
  184                      awaiting_tool_call_ids.retain(|id| id != call_id);
  185                  }
  186              }
  187              ResponseItem::Reasoning { content, .. } => {
  188                  let text = content
  189                      .as_deref()
  190                      .unwrap_or_default()
  191                      .iter()
展开 28 行逐行翻译

L1factarchitecture

06 · 共享内核仍是 turn 内多 step 的流式工具循环

源码事实

run_turn 先做预压缩、注入 skills/plugins、运行 session/user hooks并记录输入；随后循环捕获 step context、构造历史、流式 sampling。模型要求工具或有 pending input 就继续，否则运行 stop/after-agent hook 后结束。

白话解释

一次用户请求会反复经历“取固定快照—问模型—跑工具—把结果放回去”，直到模型真正收尾。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Harness 仿真共享同一个成熟执行底盘，不需要每种 Harness 重写 agent loop。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 140–183
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 184–228
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · aaf0aebf8203 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs:140–228固定提交 ↗
  140  ///
  141  /// - If the model requests a function call, we execute it and send the output
  142  ///   back to the model in the next sampling request.
  143  /// - If the model sends only an assistant message, we record it in the
  144  ///   conversation history and consider the turn complete.
  145  ///
  146  pub(crate) async fn run_turn(
  147      sess: Arc<Session>,
  148      turn_context: Arc<TurnContext>,
  149      turn_extension_data: Arc<codex_extension_api::ExtensionData>,
  150      input: Vec<TurnInput>,
  151      prewarmed_client_session: Option<ModelClientSession>,
  152      cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
  153  ) -> CodexResult<Option<String>> {
  154      let mut client_session =
  155          prewarmed_client_session.unwrap_or_else(|| sess.services.model_client.new_session());
  156      // TODO(ccunningham): Pre-turn compaction runs before context updates and the
  157      // new user message are recorded. Estimate pending incoming items (context
  158      // diffs/full reinjection + user input) and trigger compaction preemptively
  159      // when they would push the thread over the compaction threshold.
  160      if let Err(err) = run_pre_sampling_compact(&sess, &turn_context, &mut client_session).await {
  161          if matches!(err, CodexErr::TurnAborted) {
  162              return Err(err);
  163          }
  164          let error = err.to_codex_protocol_error();
  165          sess.emit_turn_error_lifecycle(turn_context.as_ref(), error.clone())
  166              .await;
  167          error!("Failed to run pre-sampling compact");
  168          return Ok(None);
  169      }
  170
  171      // run_turn owns the step used to seed context and make the first sampling request.
  172      let first_step_context = sess.capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context)).await;
  173      // Keep the exact model-visible state used by this turn and its inline compactions.
  174      let (mut world_state, display_roots) = tokio::join!(
  175          sess.record_context_updates_and_set_reference_context_item(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  176          turn_diff_display_roots(turn_context.as_ref()),
  177      );
  178
  179      let Some((injection_items, explicitly_enabled_connectors)) = build_skills_and_plugins(
  180          &sess,
  181          first_step_context.as_ref(),
  182          &input,
  183          &cancellation_token,
  184      )
  185      .await
  186      else {
  187          return Ok(None);
  188      };
  189
  190      if run_pending_session_start_hooks(&sess, &turn_context).await {
  191          return Ok(None);
  192      }
  193      let mut can_drain_pending_input = input.is_empty();
  194      if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await {
  195          return Ok(None);
  196      }
  197
  198      sess.merge_connector_selection(explicitly_enabled_connectors.clone())
  199          .await;
  200      sess.set_previous_turn_settings(Some(PreviousTurnSettings {
  201          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  202          comp_hash: turn_context.model_info.comp_hash.clone(),
  203          realtime_active: Some(turn_context.realtime_active),
  204      }))
  205      .await;
  206      for response_item in injection_items {
  207          sess.record_conversation_items(&turn_context, std::slice::from_ref(&response_item))
  208              .await;
  209      }
  210
  211      track_turn_resolved_config_analytics(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  212
  213      let mut last_agent_message: Option<String> = None;
  214      let mut stop_hook_active = false;
  215      // Although from the perspective of codex.rs, TurnDiffTracker has the lifecycle of a Task which contains
  216      // many turns, from the perspective of the user, it is a single turn.
  217      let turn_diff_tracker = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(
  218          TurnDiffTracker::with_environment_display_roots(display_roots),
  219      ));
  220
  221      // `ModelClientSession` is turn-scoped and caches WebSocket + sticky routing state, so we reuse
  222      // one instance across retries within this turn.
  223      // Pending input is drained into history before building the next model request.
  224      // However, we defer that drain until after sampling in two cases:
  225      // 1. At the start of a turn, so the fresh turn input in `input` gets sampled first.
  226      // 2. After auto-compact, when model/tool continuation needs to resume before any steer.
  227
  228      let mut next_step_context = Some(first_step_context);
  229      loop {
  230          // Note that pending_input would be something like a message the user
  231          // submitted through the UI while the model was running. Though the UI
  232          // may support this, the model might not.
  233          let pending_input = if can_drain_pending_input {
  234              sess.input_queue.get_pending_input(&sess.active_turn).await
  235          } else {
  236              Vec::new()
  237          };
  238
  239          if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &pending_input).await {
  240              break;
  241          }
  242
  243          let window_id = sess.current_window_id().await;
  244          super::rollout_budget::maybe_record_reminder(
  245              sess.as_ref(),
  246              turn_context.as_ref(),
  247              &window_id,
  248          )
  249          .await;
  250
  251          // Capture once so context, advertised tools, and tool calls share one request view.
  252          let step_context = match next_step_context.take() {
  253              Some(step_context) => step_context,
  254              None => sess.capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context)).await,
  255          };
  256          let sampling_request_result: CodexResult<_> = async {
  257              super::time_reminder::maybe_record_current_time_reminder(
  258                  sess.as_ref(),
  259                  turn_context.as_ref(),
  260                  &window_id,
  261              )
  262              .await?;
  263
  264              world_state = sess
  265                  .record_step_world_state_if_changed(&world_state, step_context.as_ref())
  266                  .await;
  267
  268              // Construct the input that we will send to the model.
  269              let sampling_request_input: Vec<ResponseItem> = async {
  270                  sess.clone_history()
  271                      .await
  272                      .for_prompt(&turn_context.model_info.input_modalities)
  273              }
  274              .instrument(trace_span!("run_turn.prepare_sampling_request_input"))
  275              .await;
  276
  277              let responses_metadata = turn_context.turn_metadata_state.to_responses_metadata(
  278                  sess.installation_id.clone(),
  279                  window_id,
  280                  CodexResponsesRequestKind::Turn,
  281              );
  282              run_sampling_request(
  283                  Arc::clone(&sess),
  284                  Arc::clone(&step_context),
  285                  Arc::clone(&turn_extension_data),
  286                  Arc::clone(&turn_diff_tracker),
  287                  &mut client_session,
  288                  &responses_metadata,
  289                  sampling_request_input,
  290                  cancellation_token.child_token(),
  291              )
  292              .await
  293          }
  294          .await;
  295          match sampling_request_result {
  296              Ok((sampling_request_output, sampling_request_input)) => {
  297                  let SamplingRequestResult {
  298                      needs_follow_up: model_needs_follow_up,
  299                      last_agent_message: sampling_request_last_agent_message,

codex-rs/core/src/session/turn.rs · 984acc698cd0当前源码 ↗
  140  /// the model replies with either:
  141  ///
  142  /// - requested function calls
  143  /// - an assistant message
  144  ///
  145  /// While it is possible for the model to return multiple of these items in a
  146  /// single sampling request, in practice, we generally one item per sampling request:
  147  ///
  148  /// - If the model requests a function call, we execute it and send the output
  149  ///   back to the model in the next sampling request.
  150  /// - If the model sends only an assistant message, we record it in the
  151  ///   conversation history and consider the turn complete.
  152  ///
  153  pub(crate) async fn run_turn(
  154      sess: Arc<Session>,
  155      turn_context: Arc<TurnContext>,
  156      turn_extension_data: Arc<codex_extension_api::ExtensionData>,
  157      input: Vec<TurnInput>,
  158      prewarmed_client_session: Option<ModelClientSession>,
  159      cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
  160  ) -> CodexResult<Option<String>> {
  161      let mut client_session =
  162          prewarmed_client_session.unwrap_or_else(|| sess.services.model_client.new_session());
  163      // TODO(ccunningham): Pre-turn compaction runs before context updates and the
  164      // new user message are recorded. Estimate pending incoming items (context
  165      // diffs/full reinjection + user input) and trigger compaction preemptively
  166      // when they would push the thread over the compaction threshold.
  167      if let Err(err) = run_pre_sampling_compact(
  168          &sess,
  169          &turn_context,
  170          &mut client_session,
  171          &cancellation_token,
  172      )
  173      .await
  174      {
  175          if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) {
  176              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  177              return Err(err);
  178          }
  179          let error = err.to_codex_protocol_error();
  180          sess.emit_turn_error_lifecycle(turn_context.as_ref(), error.clone())
  181              .await;
  182          error!("Failed to run pre-sampling compact");
  183          return Ok(None);
  184      }
  185
  186      // run_turn owns the step used to seed context and make the first sampling request.
  187      let first_step_context = match sess
  188          .capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context), &cancellation_token)
  189          .await
  190      {
  191          Ok(step_context) => step_context,
  192          Err(err) if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) => {
  193              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  194              return Err(err);
  195          }
  196          Err(err) => return Err(err),
  197      };
  198      // Keep the exact model-visible state used by this turn and its inline compactions.
  199      let (mut world_state, display_roots) = tokio::join!(
  200          sess.record_context_updates_and_set_reference_context_item(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  201          turn_diff_display_roots(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  202      );
  203
  204      let Some((injection_items, explicitly_enabled_connectors)) = build_skills_and_plugins(
  205          &sess,
  206          first_step_context.as_ref(),
  207          &input,
  208          &cancellation_token,
  209      )
  210      .await
  211      else {
  212          return Ok(None);
  213      };
  214
  215      if run_pending_session_start_hooks(&sess, &turn_context).await {
  216          return Ok(None);
  217      }
  218      let mut can_drain_pending_input = input.is_empty();
  219      if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await {
  220          return Ok(None);
  221      }
  222
  223      sess.merge_connector_selection(explicitly_enabled_connectors.clone())
  224          .await;
  225      sess.set_previous_turn_settings(Some(PreviousTurnSettings {
  226          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  227          comp_hash: turn_context.model_info.comp_hash.clone(),
  228          realtime_active: Some(turn_context.realtime_active),
  229      }))
  230      .await;
  231      for response_item in injection_items {
  232          sess.record_conversation_items(&turn_context, std::slice::from_ref(&response_item))
  233              .await;
  234      }
  235
  236      track_turn_resolved_config_analytics(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  237
  238      let mut last_agent_message: Option<String> = None;
  239      let mut stop_hook_active = false;
  240      // Although from the perspective of codex.rs, TurnDiffTracker has the lifecycle of a Task which contains
  241      // many turns, from the perspective of the user, it is a single turn.
  242      let turn_diff_tracker = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(
  243          TurnDiffTracker::with_environment_display_roots(display_roots),
  244      ));
  245
  246      // `ModelClientSession` is turn-scoped and caches WebSocket + sticky routing state, so we reuse
  247      // one instance across retries within this turn.
  248      // Pending input is drained into history before building the next model request.
  249      // However, we defer that drain until after sampling in two cases:
  250      // 1. At the start of a turn, so the fresh turn input in `input` gets sampled first.
  251      // 2. After auto-compact, when model/tool continuation needs to resume before any steer.
  252
  253      let mut next_step_context = Some(first_step_context);
  254      loop {
  255          // Note that pending_input would be something like a message the user
  256          // submitted through the UI while the model was running. Though the UI
  257          // may support this, the model might not.
  258          let pending_input = if can_drain_pending_input {
  259              sess.input_queue.get_pending_input(&sess.active_turn).await
  260          } else {
  261              Vec::new()
  262          };
  263
  264          if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &pending_input).await {
  265              break;
  266          }
  267
  268          let window_id = sess.current_window_id().await;
  269          super::rollout_budget::maybe_record_reminder(
  270              sess.as_ref(),
  271              turn_context.as_ref(),
  272              &window_id,
  273          )
  274          .await;
  275
  276          // Capture once so context, advertised tools, and tool calls share one request view.
  277          let step_context = match next_step_context.take() {
  278              Some(step_context) => step_context,
  279              None => {
  280                  sess.capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context), &cancellation_token)
  281                      .await?
  282              }
  283          };
  284          let sampling_request_result: CodexResult<_> = async {
  285              super::time_reminder::maybe_record_current_time_reminder(
  286                  sess.as_ref(),
  287                  turn_context.as_ref(),
  288                  &window_id,
  289              )
  290              .await?;
  291
  292              world_state = sess
  293                  .record_step_world_state_if_changed(&world_state, step_context.as_ref())
  294                  .await;
  295
  296              // Construct the input that we will send to the model.
  297              let sampling_request_input: Vec<ResponseItem> = async {
  298                  sess.clone_history()
  299                      .await
07
L1factarchitecture

07 · step 快照让上下文、工具清单与工具执行看到同一世界

源码事实

每个循环只 capture 一次 StepContext，注释明确要求 context、advertised tools 和 tool calls 共享同一 request view；world state 变化被记录到 rollout，下一 step 才重新采样环境。

白话解释

模型看到的工具说明和真正执行工具时的权限/目录不会在同一步里偷偷漂移。

对自研 Harness 的含义

降低并发配置变化导致的 TOCTOU 类语义错位；动态变更需要等下一 step 生效。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 243–266
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 267–292
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  363                          CompactionReason::ContextLimit,

  324              Ok((sampling_request_output, sampling_request_input)) => {
  325                  let SamplingRequestResult {
  326                      needs_follow_up: model_needs_follow_up,
  327                      last_agent_message: sampling_request_last_agent_message,
  328                  } = sampling_request_output;
  329                  if model_needs_follow_up {
  330                      sess.input_queue
  331                          .accept_mailbox_delivery_for_current_turn(
  332                              &sess.active_turn,
  333                              &turn_context.sub_id,
  334                          )
  335                          .await;
  336                  }
  337                  can_drain_pending_input = true;
  338                  let (has_pending_input, token_status) = async {
  339                      let has_pending_input =
  340                          sess.input_queue.has_pending_input(&sess.active_turn).await;
  341                      let token_status = super::context_window::context_window_token_status(
  342                          sess.as_ref(),
  343                          turn_context.as_ref(),
  344                      )
  345                      .await;
  346                      (has_pending_input, token_status)
  347                  }
  348                  .instrument(trace_span!("run_turn.collect_post_sampling_state"))
  349                  .await;
  350                  let needs_follow_up = model_needs_follow_up || has_pending_input;
  351                  let token_limit_reached = token_status.token_limit_reached;
  352
  353                  trace!(
  354                      turn_id = %turn_context.sub_id,
  355                      total_usage_tokens = token_status.active_context_tokens,
  356                      auto_compact_scope_tokens = token_status.auto_compact_scope_tokens,
  357                      auto_compact_scope_limit = ?token_status.auto_compact_scope_limit,
  358                      auto_compact_limit_scope = ?turn_context.config.model_auto_compact_token_limit_scope,
  359                      auto_compact_window_prefill_tokens = ?token_status.auto_compact_window_prefill_tokens,
  360                      full_context_window_limit = ?token_status.full_context_window_limit,
  361                      full_context_window_limit_reached = token_status.full_context_window_limit_reached,
  362                      token_limit_reached,
  363                      model_needs_follow_up,
03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

模型与上下文

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factmodelContext

01 · 历史管理维护调用配对、多模态能力和可见 token 成本

源码事实

ContextManager 记录 ResponseItem 后按输入模态规范化：补齐缺失 call output、删除孤儿 output、剥离不支持的图片/音频；工具输出按策略截断，并用模型可见字节估算 token。

白话解释

上下文不是简单 messages 数组；它会修账，保证工具订单和回执成对，并避免把模型根本不能看的媒体继续塞回去。

对自研 Harness 的含义

恢复和压缩后的协议更稳定；token 估算是启发式下界，不等于 provider tokenizer。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 40–112
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 113–186
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   40  pub(crate) struct ContextManager {
   41      /// The oldest items are at the beginning of the vector. Snapshots share the vector until a
   42      /// caller needs to mutate it, avoiding deep copies for read-only history consumers.

   44      /// Bumped whenever history is rewritten, such as compaction or rollback.

   48      /// settings update items.

   50      /// This is the baseline for the next regular model turn, and may already
   51      /// match the current turn after context updates are persisted.
   52      ///

   54      /// baseline and emits a full reinjection of context state. Rollback may

   95          let snapshot = world_state.snapshot();

   98          let rollout_item = self.world_state_baseline.as_ref().map_or_else(

  123      /// `items` is ordered from oldest to newest.
  124      pub(crate) fn record_items<I>(&mut self, items: I, policy: TruncationPolicy)
  125      where
  126          I: IntoIterator,
  127          I::Item: std::ops::Deref<Target = ResponseItem>,
  128      {
  129          for item in items {
  130              let item_ref = item.deref();

  132                  continue;
  133              }
  134
  135              let processed = Self::process_item(item_ref, policy);
  136              Arc::make_mut(&mut self.items).push(processed);
  137          }
  138      }

  164      pub(crate) fn estimate_token_count(&self, turn_context: &TurnContext) -> Option<i64> {
  165          let model_info = &turn_context.model_info;
  166          let personality = turn_context.personality.or(turn_context.config.personality);
  167          let base_instructions = BaseInstructions {
  168              text: model_info.get_model_instructions(personality),
  169          };
  170          self.estimate_token_count_with_base_instructions(&base_instructions)

  173      pub(crate) fn estimate_token_count_with_base_instructions(
  174          &self,
  175          base_instructions: &BaseInstructions,
  176      ) -> Option<i64> {
  177          let base_tokens =
  178              i64::try_from(approx_token_count(&base_instructions.text)).unwrap_or(i64::MAX);
  179
  180          let items_tokens = self
  181              .items
  182              .iter()
  183              .map(estimate_item_token_count)
  184              .fold(0i64, i64::saturating_add);
  185
  186          Some(base_tokens.saturating_add(items_tokens))
  187      }

  189      pub(crate) fn remove_first_item(&mut self) {
  190          if !self.items.is_empty() {
  191              // Remove the oldest item (front of the list). Items are ordered from
  192              // oldest → newest, so index 0 is the first entry recorded.
  193              let items = Arc::make_mut(&mut self.items);
  194              let removed = items.remove(0);
  195              // If the removed item participates in a call/output pair, also remove
  196              // its corresponding counterpart to keep the invariants intact without
  197              // running a full normalization pass.
  198              normalize::remove_corresponding_for(items, &removed);
  199              self.world_state_baseline = None;
  200          }
  201      }
  202
  203      pub(crate) fn replace(&mut self, items: Vec<ResponseItem>) {
  204          self.items = Arc::new(items);
  205          self.history_version = self.history_version.saturating_add(1);
  206          self.world_state_baseline = None;
  207      }
  208
  209      /// Drop the last `num_turns` instruction turns from this history.
  210      ///
  211      /// Instruction turns are history messages that should behave like a new prompt boundary:
  212      /// ordinary user messages and structured assistant inter-agent instructions.
  213      ///
  214      /// This mirrors thread-rollback semantics:
  215      /// - `num_turns == 0` is a no-op
  216      /// - if there are no user turns, this is a no-op
  217      /// - if `num_turns` exceeds the number of user turns, all user turns are dropped while
  218      ///   preserving any items that occurred before the first user message.
  219      ///
  220      /// If rollback trims a pre-turn developer message that mixes contextual fragments with
  221      /// persistent developer text from `build_initial_context`, this also clears
  222      /// `reference_context_item`. The surviving history no longer contains the full bundle that
  223      /// established the prior baseline, so future turns must fall back to full reinjection instead
  224      /// of diffing against stale state.
  225      pub(crate) fn drop_last_n_user_turns(&mut self, num_turns: u32) {
  226          if num_turns == 0 {
  227              return;
  228          }
  229
  230          let snapshot = self.items.clone();
  231          let user_positions = user_message_positions(&snapshot);
  232          let Some(&first_instruction_turn_idx) = user_positions.first() else {
  233              self.replace(Arc::unwrap_or_clone(snapshot));
  234              return;
  235          };
  236
  237          let n_from_end = usize::try_from(num_turns).unwrap_or(usize::MAX);
  238          let mut cut_idx = if n_from_end >= user_positions.len() {
  239              first_instruction_turn_idx
  240          } else {
  241              user_positions[user_positions.len() - n_from_end]
  242          };
  243
  244          cut_idx =
  245              self.trim_pre_turn_context_updates(&snapshot, first_instruction_turn_idx, cut_idx);
  246
  247          self.replace(snapshot[..cut_idx].to_vec());
  248      }
  249
  250      pub(crate) fn update_token_info(
  251          &mut self,
  252          usage: &TokenUsage,
  253          model_context_window: Option<i64>,
  254      ) {
  255          self.token_info = TokenUsageInfo::new_or_append(
  256              &self.token_info,
  257              &Some(usage.clone()),

02
L1factmodelContext

02 · 压缩是 Harness-aware 的，而不是统一摘要模板

源码事实

压缩先用当前 Harness 生成摘要请求，然后 build_harness_compacted_history：ZCode、Claude Code bare 等可走各自 replacement history；普通路径用 summary prefix。还会从 harness read 调用重建相关文件内容，并识别 no-truncate 标记。

白话解释

换了驾驶舱，压缩后的“交接便笺”也要换写法，否则目标模型会读不懂或丢失刚看过的文件。

对自研 Harness 的含义

长会话兼容性更好；每新增 Harness 都必须测试压缩与恢复，否则短任务能跑、长任务会失真。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 299–343
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 344–389
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  435
  436      pub(crate) async fn track(
  437          self,
  438          sess: &Session,
  439          status: CompactionStatus,
  440          codex_error: Option<&CodexErr>,
  441          details: CompactionAnalyticsDetails,
  442      ) {
  443          let CompactionAnalyticsDetails {
  444              active_context_tokens_before,
  445              retained_image_count,
  446              compaction_summary_tokens,
  447              cached_input_tokens,
  448              cache_write_input_tokens,
  449          } = details;
  450          let active_context_tokens_before =
  451              active_context_tokens_before.unwrap_or(self.active_context_tokens_before);
  452          let active_context_tokens_after = sess.get_total_token_usage().await;
  453          sess.services
  454              .analytics_events_client
  455              .track_compaction(CodexCompactionEvent {

  299          match attempt_result {
  300              Ok(()) => {
  301                  break;
  302              }
  303              Err(err)
  304                  if matches!(
  305                      err.details(),
  306                      CodexErrorDetails::Interrupted | CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted
  307                  ) =>
  308              {
  309                  return Err(err);
  310              }
  311              Err(e) if matches!(e.details(), CodexErrorDetails::SessionBudgetExceeded) => {
  312                  sess.track_turn_codex_error(turn_context.as_ref(), &e);
  313                  let event = EventMsg::Error(e.to_error_event(/*message_prefix*/ None));
  314                  sess.send_event(&turn_context, event).await;
  315                  return Err(e);
  316              }
  317              Err(e) if matches!(e.details(), CodexErrorDetails::ContextWindowExceeded) => {
  318                  if turn_input_len > 1 {
  319                      // Trim from the beginning to preserve cache (prefix-based) and keep recent messages intact.
  320                      error!(
  321                          "Context window exceeded while compacting; removing oldest history item. Error: {e}"
  322                      );
  323                      history.remove_first_item();
  324                      retries = 0;
  325                      continue;
  326                  }
  327                  sess.set_total_tokens_full(turn_context.as_ref()).await;

  329                  let event = EventMsg::Error(e.to_error_event(/*message_prefix*/ None));
  330                  sess.send_event(&turn_context, event).await;
  331                  return Err(e);
  332              }
  333              Err(e) => {
  334                  if retries < max_retries {
  335                      retries += 1;
  336                      let delay = backoff(retries);
  337                      sess.notify_stream_error(
  338                          turn_context.as_ref(),
  339                          format!("Reconnecting... {retries}/{max_retries}"),
  340                          e,
  341                      )
  342                      .await;
  343                      tokio::time::sleep(delay).await;
  344                      continue;
  345                  } else {
  346                      sess.track_turn_codex_error(turn_context.as_ref(), &e);
  347                      let event = EventMsg::Error(e.to_error_event(/*message_prefix*/ None));
  348                      sess.send_event(&turn_context, event).await;
  349                      return Err(e);
  350                  }
  351              }
  352          }
  353      }

  356      let history_items = history_snapshot.raw_items();
  357      let summary_suffix = get_last_assistant_message_from_turn(history_items).unwrap_or_default();
  358      let user_messages = collect_user_messages(history_items);
  359

  361          turn_context.as_ref(),
  362          history_items,
  363          &user_messages,
  364          &summary_suffix,

  367          // This replacement history skips `record_conversation_items`; only the appended summary
  368          // belongs to this compaction turn.
  369          summary_item.set_turn_id_if_missing(&turn_context.sub_id);
  370      }
  371      let (window_number, window_ids) = sess.advance_auto_compact_window().await;
  372
  373      let (initial_context, world_state_baseline) =
  374          build_compaction_initial_context(sess.as_ref(), &initial_context_injection).await;
  375      if !initial_context.is_empty() {
  376          new_history =
  377              insert_initial_context_before_last_real_user_or_summary(new_history, initial_context);
  378      }
  379      let reference_context_item = match initial_context_injection {
  380          InitialContextInjection::DoNotInject => None,
  381          InitialContextInjection::BeforeLastUserMessage { .. } => {
  382              Some(turn_context.to_turn_context_item())
  383          }
  384      };
  385      sess.replace_compacted_history(
  386          new_history,
  387          reference_context_item,
  388          world_state_baseline,
  389          CompactedHistoryMetadata {
  390              message: summary_text,
  391              window_number,
  392              window_ids,
  393          },

  399          .await;

  403      sess.send_event(&turn_context, warning).await;

05
DIMENSION · TOOLS

工具注册与执行

4 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1facttools

01 · 模型可见工具与内部可分发 runtime 分离

源码事实

Tool plan 先按 exposure 去重、生成模型可见 spec，再由全部 runtimes 建 ToolRegistry；dispatch-only handler 可以不可见但仍接住兼容工具名。Code mode 又把允许的工具 schema 包进 execute/wait 两个工具。

白话解释

给模型看的菜单和厨房真正能接的订单不是同一张表；旧别名可以藏在后厨，不污染新模型的菜单。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼容多 Harness 时能避免重复工具；隐藏 handler 仍属于攻击面，必须受同一权限检查。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 240–253
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 254–268
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  271  fn spec_for_model_request(
  272      turn_context: &TurnContext,
  273      exposure: ToolExposure,
  274      tool_name: &ToolName,
  275      spec: ToolSpec,
  276  ) -> ToolSpec {
  277      let tool_mode = effective_tool_mode(turn_context);
  278      if matches!(tool_mode, ToolMode::CodeMode | ToolMode::CodeModeOnly)
  279          && exposure != ToolExposure::DirectModelOnly
  280          && !is_excluded_from_code_mode(turn_context, tool_name)
  281          && codex_code_mode::is_code_mode_nested_tool(spec.name())
  282      {
  283          codex_tools::augment_tool_spec_for_code_mode(spec)
  284      } else {
  285          spec
  286      }
  287  }

  289  #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
  290  fn hosted_model_tool_specs(context: &CoreToolPlanContext<'_>) -> Vec<ToolSpec> {
  291      let turn_context = context.step_context.turn.as_ref();
  292      // Responses Lite accepts schemas for client-executed tools, not hosted Responses tools.
  293      if turn_context.model_info.use_responses_lite {
  294          return Vec::new();
  295      }
  296
  297      let mut specs = Vec::new();
  298      let standalone_web_search_available = standalone_web_search_enabled(turn_context)
  299          && context
  300              .extension_tool_executors
  301              .iter()
  302              .any(|executor| executor.tool_name() == ToolName::namespaced("web", "run"));
  303      // `Some(Cached/Live/Disabled)` are the options for mode when standalone search is unavailable
  304      // and the provider supports hosted search. `None` prevents emitting a hosted search tool.
  305      let web_search_mode = (!standalone_web_search_available
  306          && turn_context.provider.capabilities().web_search)
  307          .then_some(turn_context.config.web_search_mode.value());
  308      let web_search_config = web_search_mode
  309          .as_ref()
  310          .and(turn_context.config.web_search_config.as_ref());
  311      if let Some(hosted_web_search_tool) = create_web_search_tool(WebSearchToolOptions {
  312          web_search_mode,
  313          web_search_config,
  314          web_search_tool_type: turn_context.model_info.web_search_tool_type,
  315      }) {
  316          specs.push(hosted_web_search_tool);
  317      }
  318      specs
  319  }

  329          && features.enabled(Feature::Plugins)

  247  #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
  248  fn build_model_visible_specs_and_registry(
  249      turn_context: &TurnContext,
  250      planned_tools: PlannedTools,
  251  ) -> (Vec<ToolSpec>, ToolRegistry) {
  252      let PlannedTools {
  253          runtimes,
  254          hosted_specs,
  255      } = planned_tools;
  256      let mut specs = Vec::new();
  257      let mut seen_tool_names = HashSet::new();
  258      for runtime in &runtimes {
  259          let tool_name = runtime.tool_name();
  260          if !seen_tool_names.insert(tool_name.clone()) {
  261              continue;
  262          }
  263          let exposure = runtime.exposure();
  264          if exposure.is_direct() && !is_hidden_by_code_mode_only(turn_context, &tool_name, exposure)
  265          {
  266              let spec = runtime.spec();
  267              specs.push(spec_for_model_request(
  268                  turn_context,
  269                  exposure,
  270                  &tool_name,
  271                  spec,
  272              ));
  273          }
  274      }
  275      specs.extend(hosted_specs);
  276
  277      let registry = ToolRegistry::from_tools(runtimes);
  278      let model_visible_specs = merge_into_namespaces(specs)
  279          .into_iter()
  280          .filter(|spec| {
  281              namespace_tools_enabled(turn_context) || !matches!(spec, ToolSpec::Namespace(_))
  282          })
  283          .collect();
  284
  285      (model_visible_specs, registry)
  286  }

02
L1facttools

02 · Harness aliases 将十余套工具名收敛到共享执行器

源码事实

HarnessAliasHandler 覆盖 Bash/Read/Write/Edit/Glob/Grep、Claude task、Kimi goal/cron、DeepSeek diagnostics/git/file、OpenCode task/todo、ZCode plan/skill 等；spec plan 按当前 Harness 添加可见或 dispatch-only alias。

白话解释

Claude 叫 Read、Pi 叫 read、OpenCode 叫 task，都可以落到同一套真实能力上。

对自研 Harness 的含义

跨 Harness 复用强；别名层很大，参数语义和错误文本需要逐 Harness 回归。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 102–177
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 178–254
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L1facttools

03 · Harness 写文件也进入统一 FileChange lifecycle

源码事实

Write/Edit alias 先读取旧内容构建 Add/Update unified diff，发 Started，再执行写入；成功发 Completed，失败发 Failed。事件通过 TurnItem::FileChange 进入会话与 rollout，而不是只返回一句工具文本。

白话解释

即使模型说的是别家 Agent 的 Write/Edit，前端仍能看到正式的文件改动卡片、diff、成功或失败状态。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼容层不会绕过观测与审计；这是当前提交的主变更点。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1749–1784
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1785–1821
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 1749  #[derive(Deserialize)]
 1750  struct WriteArgs {
 1751      #[serde(alias = "file_path", alias = "filePath")]
 1752      path: String,
 1753      content: String,
 1754      #[serde(default)]
 1755      mode: Option<String>,
 1756  }
 1757
 1758  #[derive(Clone, Copy)]
 1759  enum HarnessFileChangeStage<'a> {
 1760      Started,
 1761      Completed,
 1762      Failed(&'a str),
 1763  }
 1764
 1765  fn harness_file_change(
 1766      path: &Path,
 1767      previous_content: Option<&str>,
 1768      updated_content: &str,
 1769  ) -> HashMap<PathBuf, FileChange> {
 1770      let change = match previous_content {
 1771          Some(previous_content) => FileChange::Update {
 1772              unified_diff: similar::TextDiff::from_lines(previous_content, updated_content)
 1773                  .unified_diff()
 1774                  .context_radius(3)
 1775                  .to_string(),
 1776              move_path: None,
 1777          },
 1778          None => FileChange::Add {
 1779              content: updated_content.to_string(),
 1780          },
 1781      };
 1782      HashMap::from([(path.to_path_buf(), change)])
 1783  }
 1784
 1785  async fn emit_harness_file_change(
 1786      invocation: &ToolInvocation,
 1787      changes: &HashMap<PathBuf, FileChange>,
 1788      stage: HarnessFileChangeStage<'_>,
 1789  ) {
 1790      let (status, auto_approved, stderr) = match stage {
 1791          HarnessFileChangeStage::Started => (None, Some(true), None),
 1792          HarnessFileChangeStage::Completed => (Some(PatchApplyStatus::Completed), None, None),
 1793          HarnessFileChangeStage::Failed(message) => (
 1794              Some(PatchApplyStatus::Failed),
 1795              None,
 1796              Some(message.to_string()),
 1797          ),
 1798      };
 1799      let item = TurnItem::FileChange(FileChangeItem {
 1800          id: invocation.call_id.clone(),
 1801          changes: changes.clone(),
 1802          status,
 1803          auto_approved,
 1804          stdout: None,
 1805          stderr,
 1806      });
 1807      match stage {
 1808          HarnessFileChangeStage::Started => {
 1809              invocation
 1810                  .session
 1811                  .emit_turn_item_started(&invocation.turn, &item)
 1812                  .await;
 1813          }
 1814          HarnessFileChangeStage::Completed | HarnessFileChangeStage::Failed(_) => {
 1815              invocation
 1816                  .session
 1817                  .emit_turn_item_completed(&invocation.turn, item)
 1818                  .await;
 1819          }
 1820      }
 1821  }
 1822
 1823  async fn handle_write(
 1824      invocation: ToolInvocation,
 1825  ) -> Result<Box<dyn ToolOutput>, FunctionCallError> {
 1826      let arguments = function_arguments(&invocation.payload)?;
 1827      let args: WriteArgs = parse_arguments(arguments)?;
 1828      let path = harness_fs::checked_write_path(&invocation, &args.path, "Write")?;
 1829      if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
 1830          std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
 1831              .map_err(|err| FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(format!("Write failed: {err}")))?;
 1832      }
 1833      let previous_content = std::fs::read(&path)
 1834          .ok()
 1835          .map(|content| String::from_utf8_lossy(&content).into_owned());
 1836      let updated_content = if args.mode.as_deref() == Some("append") {
 1837          format!(
 1838              "{}{}",
 1839              previous_content.as_deref().unwrap_or_default(),
 1840              args.content
 1841          )
 1842      } else {
 1843          args.content.clone()
 1844      };
 1845      let changes = harness_file_change(&path, previous_content.as_deref(), &updated_content);
 1846      emit_harness_file_change(&invocation, &changes, HarnessFileChangeStage::Started).await;
 1847      let bytes_written = args.content.len();
 1848      let write_result = match args.mode.as_deref() {
 1849          Some("append") => {
 1850              use std::io::Write as _;
 1851              std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
 1852                  .create(true)
 1853                  .append(true)
 1854                  .open(&path)
 1855                  .and_then(|mut file| file.write_all(args.content.as_bytes()))
 1856          }
 1857          _ => std::fs::write(&path, &args.content),
 1858      };
 1859      if let Err(err) = write_result {
 1860          let message = format!("Write failed: {err}");
 1861          emit_harness_file_change(
 1862              &invocation,
 1863              &changes,
 1864              HarnessFileChangeStage::Failed(&message),
 1865          )
 1866          .await;
 1867          return Err(FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(message));
 1868      }
 1869      emit_harness_file_change(&invocation, &changes, HarnessFileChangeStage::Completed).await;
 1870      let message = if is_zcode(&invocation) {
 1871          record_zcode_current_file(&invocation, &path);
 1872          record_zcode_current_file_hash(
 1873              &invocation,
 1874              &path,
 1875              zcode_file_hash(args.content.as_bytes()),
 1876          );
 1877          format!(
 1878              "File created successfully at: {} (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)",
 1879              path.display()
 1880          )
 1881      } else if invocation.tool_name.name == "write" {
 1882          if is_opencode(&invocation) {
 1883              "Wrote file successfully.".to_string()
 1884          } else if is_pi(&invocation) {
 1885              format!(
 1886                  "Successfully wrote {bytes_written} bytes to {}",
 1887                  display_model_path(&invocation, &path)
 1888              )
 1889          } else {
 1890              format!(
 1891                  "Successfully wrote {bytes_written} bytes to {}",
 1892                  path.display()
