04
L1facttools

04 · 工具并发用读写锁表达，而不是一律并发

源码事实

ToolCallRuntime 查询 handler 是否支持并发；并发工具取得共享 read lock，写入/交互等排他工具取得 write lock，因此同组读工具可并行，任何排他工具会等待并阻塞后续组。

白话解释

多个搜索可以一起跑，但写文件或问用户会像闸门一样独占执行通道。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼顾吞吐和副作用顺序；alias 自己也声明 Edit/Write/AskUserQuestion 不并行。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 42–89
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 90–138
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · aaf0aebf8203 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/core/src/tools/parallel.rs:42–138固定提交 ↗
   42  pub(crate) struct ToolCallRuntime {
   43      router: Arc<ToolRouter>,
   44      session: Arc<Session>,
   45      // Tool calls may run later, so retain the step whose tool list advertised them.
   46      step_context: Arc<StepContext>,
   47      tracker: SharedTurnDiffTracker,
   48      parallel_execution: Arc<RwLock<()>>,
   49  }
   50
   51  impl ToolCallRuntime {
   52      pub(crate) fn new(
   53          router: Arc<ToolRouter>,
   54          session: Arc<Session>,
   55          step_context: Arc<StepContext>,
   56          tracker: SharedTurnDiffTracker,
   57      ) -> Self {
   58          Self {
   59              router,
   60              session,
   61              step_context,
   62              tracker,
   63              parallel_execution: Arc::new(RwLock::new(())),
   64          }
   65      }
   66
   67      pub(crate) fn create_diff_consumer(
   68          &self,
   69          tool_name: &codex_tools::ToolName,
   70      ) -> Option<Box<dyn ToolArgumentDiffConsumer>> {
   71          self.router.create_diff_consumer(tool_name)
   72      }
   73
   74      #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
   75      pub(crate) fn handle_tool_call(
   76          self,
   77          call: ToolCall,
   78          cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
   79      ) -> impl std::future::Future<Output = Result<ResponseInputItem, CodexErr>> {
   80          let error_call = call.clone();
   81          let future =
   82              self.handle_tool_call_with_source(call, ToolCallSource::Direct, cancellation_token);
   83          async move {
   84              match future.await {
   85                  Ok(response) => Ok(response.into_response()),
   86                  Err(FunctionCallError::Fatal(message)) => Err(CodexErr::Fatal(message)),
   87                  Err(other) => Ok(Self::failure_response(error_call, other)),
   88              }
   89          }
   90          .in_current_span()
   91      }
   92
   93      #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
   94      pub(crate) fn handle_tool_call_with_source(
   95          self,
   96          call: ToolCall,
   97          source: ToolCallSource,
   98          cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
   99      ) -> impl std::future::Future<Output = Result<AnyToolResult, FunctionCallError>> {
  100          let supports_parallel = self.router.tool_supports_parallel(&call);
  101          let router = Arc::clone(&self.router);
  102          let session = Arc::clone(&self.session);
  103          let step_context = Arc::clone(&self.step_context);
  104          let turn = Arc::clone(&step_context.turn);
  105          let tracker = Arc::clone(&self.tracker);
  106          let lock = Arc::clone(&self.parallel_execution);
  107          let invocation_cancellation_token = cancellation_token.clone();
  108          let wait_for_runtime_cancellation = self.router.tool_waits_for_runtime_cancellation(&call);
  109          let started = Instant::now();
  110          let tool_call_timing_guard =
  111              ToolCallTimingGuard::capture(started, &session.thread_id, &turn.sub_id, &call, &source);
  112          let execution_started_at = tool_call_timing_guard
  113              .as_ref()
  114              .map(|timing| Arc::clone(&timing.execution_started_at));
  115          let abort_session = Arc::clone(&session);
  116          let abort_source = source.clone();
  117          let abort_turn = Arc::clone(&turn);
  118          let terminal_outcome_reached = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
  119          let dispatch_terminal_outcome_reached = Arc::clone(&terminal_outcome_reached);
  120          let dispatch_call = call.clone();
  121
  122          let dispatch_span = trace_span!(
  123              "dispatch_tool_call_with_code_mode_result",
  124              otel.name = %call.tool_name,
  125              tool_name = %call.tool_name,
  126              call_id = call.call_id.as_str(),
  127              aborted = false,
  128          );
  129          let abort_dispatch_span = dispatch_span.clone();
  130
  131          let mut dispatch_handle: AbortOnDropHandle<Result<AnyToolResult, FunctionCallError>> =
  132              AbortOnDropHandle::new(tokio::spawn(async move {
  133                  let _guard = if supports_parallel {
  134                      Either::Left(lock.read().await)
  135                  } else {
  136                      Either::Right(lock.write().await)
  137                  };
  138                  // Admission through the parallel-execution gate marks the end
  139                  // of dispatch waiting and the start of handler execution.
  140                  if let Some(execution_started_at) = execution_started_at {
  141                      let _ = execution_started_at.set(Instant::now());
  142                  }
  143
  144                  router
  145                      .dispatch_tool_call_with_terminal_outcome(
  146                          session,
  147                          step_context,
  148                          invocation_cancellation_token,
  149                          tracker,
  150                          dispatch_call,
  151                          source,
  152                          dispatch_terminal_outcome_reached,
  153                      )
  154                      .instrument(dispatch_span.clone())
  155                      .await
  156              }));
  157
  158          async move {
  159              let _tool_call_timing_guard = tool_call_timing_guard;
  160              tokio::select! {
  161                  res = &mut dispatch_handle => res.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?,
  162                  _ = cancellation_token.cancelled() => {
  163                      if terminal_outcome_reached.load(Ordering::Acquire) || dispatch_handle.is_finished() {
  164                          dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?
  165                      } else {
  166                          let secs = started.elapsed().as_secs_f32().max(0.1);
  167                          abort_dispatch_span.record("aborted", true);
  168                          if wait_for_runtime_cancellation {
  169                              if terminal_outcome_reached.swap(true, Ordering::AcqRel) {
  170                                  return dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?;
  171                              }
  172                              // The abort owns the terminal outcome; await only so
  173                              // the runtime can finish process teardown.
  174                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  175                                  Ok(_) => {}
  176                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  177                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  178                              }
  179                          } else {
  180                              dispatch_handle.abort();
  181                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  182                                  Ok(result) => return result,
  183                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  184                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  185                              }
  186                          }
  187                          let response = Self::aborted_response(&call, secs);
  188                          notify_tool_aborted(
  189                              abort_session.as_ref(),
  190                              abort_turn.as_ref(),
  191                              call.call_id.as_str(),
  192                              &call.tool_name,
  193                              abort_source,
  194                          )
  195                          .await;
  196                          Ok(response)
  197                      }
  198                  },
  199              }
  200          }
  201          .in_current_span()
  202      }
  203  }
  204
  205  impl ToolCallRuntime {
  206      fn tool_task_join_error(err: JoinError) -> FunctionCallError {
  207          FunctionCallError::Fatal(format!("tool task failed to receive: {err:?}"))
  208      }
  209
展开 28 行逐行翻译

06
DIMENSION · CONNECTORS

连接器、插件与 MCP

4 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factconnectors

01 · MCP、Apps、Plugins、Extensions 都在 step 工具计划中受快照控制

源码事实

turn 开始根据用户明确提及的 plugins/apps 构建注入；step context 持有 MCP 配置快照，spec plan 再把 MCP resource、extension、dynamic tools 与 core tools 合并，模型可见 spec 和实际 registry 同步生成。

白话解释

连接器不是随时从全局表里飘进来，而是在这一轮、这一步被拍成快照后再装进工具箱。

对自研 Harness 的含义

可复现性和权限边界较清楚；插件配置变化通常下一 step 才生效。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 546–597
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 598–650
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  553      if crate::guardian::is_guardian_reviewer_source(&turn_context.session_source) {
  554          return Some((Vec::new(), HashSet::new()));
  555      }

  563          .flatten()

  611      };

  707      injection_items.extend(extension_injection_items);
  708      Some((injection_items, explicitly_enabled_connectors))
  709  }

  567  #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
  568  pub(crate) async fn run_hooks_and_record_inputs(
  569      sess: &Arc<Session>,
  570      turn_context: &Arc<TurnContext>,
  571      input: &[TurnInput],
  572  ) -> bool {
  573      let mut blocked_input = false;
  574      let mut accepted_user_input = false;
  575      for input_item in input {
  576          let hook_outcome = inspect_pending_input(sess, turn_context, input_item).await;
  577          if hook_outcome.should_stop {
  578              blocked_input = true;
  579              record_additional_contexts(sess, turn_context, hook_outcome.additional_contexts).await;
  580          } else {
  581              if matches!(input_item, TurnInput::UserInput { content, .. } if !content.is_empty()) {
  582                  accepted_user_input = true;
  583              }
  584              record_pending_input(
  585                  sess,
  586                  turn_context,
  587                  input_item.clone(),
  588                  hook_outcome.additional_contexts,
  589              )
  590              .await;
  591          }
  592      }
  593      blocked_input && !accepted_user_input
  594  }

  596  #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
  597  async fn build_skills_and_plugins(
  598      sess: &Arc<Session>,
  599      step_context: &StepContext,
  600      input: &[TurnInput],
  601      cancellation_token: &CancellationToken,
  602  ) -> Option<(Vec<ResponseItem>, HashSet<String>)> {
  603      let turn_context = step_context.turn.as_ref();
  604      // Guardian input embeds the parent transcript as untrusted evidence. Do not interpret skill or
  605      // plugin mentions from that generated prompt as requests to inject additional instructions.
  606      if crate::guardian::is_guardian_reviewer_source(&turn_context.session_source) {
  607          return Some((Vec::new(), HashSet::new()));
  608      }
  609
  610      let user_input = input
  611          .iter()
  612          .filter_map(|item| match item {
  613              TurnInput::UserInput { content, .. } => Some(content.as_slice()),
  614              TurnInput::ResponseItem(_) | TurnInput::InterAgentCommunication(_) => None,
  615          })
  616          .flatten()
  617          .cloned()
  618          .collect::<Vec<_>>();
  619      let tracking = build_track_events_context(
  620          turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  621          sess.thread_id.to_string(),
  622          turn_context.sub_id.clone(),
  623          turn_context.originator.clone(),
  624      );
  625      let loaded_plugins = sess
  626          .services
  627          .plugins_manager
  628          .plugins_for_config(&turn_context.config.plugins_config_input())
  629          .await;
  630      // Structured plugin:// mentions are resolved from the current session's
  631      // enabled plugins, then converted into turn-scoped guidance below.
  632      let mentioned_plugins =
  633          collect_explicit_plugin_mentions(&user_input, loaded_plugins.capability_summaries());
  634      let connector_snapshot = step_context.mcp.config().connector_snapshot.clone();
  635      let mcp_tools = if turn_context.apps_enabled() || !mentioned_plugins.is_empty() {
  636          // Plugin mentions need raw MCP/app inventory even when app tools
  637          // are normally hidden so we can describe the plugin's currently
  638          // usable capabilities for this turn.
  639          step_context.mcp.tools().to_vec()
  640      } else {
  641          Vec::new()
  642      };
  643      let available_connectors = if turn_context.apps_enabled() {
  644          let connectors = codex_connectors::merge::merge_plugin_connectors_with_accessible(
  645              connector_snapshot
  646                  .connector_ids()
  647                  .iter()
  648                  .map(|connector_id| connector_id.0.clone()),
  649              connectors::accessible_connectors_from_mcp_tools(&mcp_tools),
  650          );
  651          connectors::with_app_enabled_state(connectors, &turn_context.config)
  652      } else {
  653          Vec::new()
  654      };
  655      let skills_outcome = turn_context.turn_skills.snapshot.outcome();
  656      let connector_slug_counts = build_connector_slug_counts(&available_connectors);
  657      let extension_injection_items =
  658          build_extension_turn_input_items(sess, step_context, &user_input, cancellation_token)
  659              .await?;
  660      let skill_name_counts_lower =
  661          build_skill_name_counts(&skills_outcome.skills, &skills_outcome.disabled_paths).1;
  662      let mentioned_skills = collect_explicit_skill_mentions(
  663          &user_input,
  664          &skills_outcome.skills,
  665          &skills_outcome.disabled_paths,
  666          &connector_slug_counts,
  667      );
  668      maybe_prompt_and_install_mcp_dependencies(
  669          sess,
  670          turn_context,
  671          cancellation_token,
  672          &mentioned_skills,
  673          Some(sess.mcp_elicitation_reviewer()),
  674      )
  675      .await;
  676
  677      let injected_host_skill_prompts = turn_context
  678          .extension_data
  679          .get::<InjectedHostSkillPrompts>();
  680      let SkillInjections {
  681          items: skill_injections,
  682          warnings: skill_warnings,
  683      } = build_skill_injections(
  684          &mentioned_skills,
  685          Some(skills_outcome),
  686          Some(&turn_context.session_telemetry),
  687          &sess.services.analytics_events_client,
  688          tracking.clone(),
  689      )
  690      .await;
  691
  692      for message in skill_warnings {
  693          sess.send_event(turn_context, EventMsg::Warning(WarningEvent { message }))
  694              .await;
  695      }
  696
  697      let skill_items: Vec<ResponseItem> = skill_injections
  698          .iter()
  699          .map(|skill| ContextualUserFragment::into(crate::context::SkillInstructions::from(skill)))
  700          .collect();
  701      let skill_connector_ids = collect_explicit_app_ids_from_skill_items(
  702          &skill_items,
  703          &available_connectors,
  704          &skill_name_counts_lower,
  705      );
  706      let plugin_items =
  707          build_plugin_injections(&mentioned_plugins, &mcp_tools, &available_connectors);
  708      let mut explicitly_enabled_connectors = collect_explicit_app_ids(&user_input);
  709      explicitly_enabled_connectors.extend(skill_connector_ids);
  710      let connector_names_by_id = available_connectors
  711          .iter()
  712          .map(|connector| (connector.id.as_str(), connector.name.as_str()))
  713          .collect::<HashMap<&str, &str>>();
  714      let mentioned_app_invocations = explicitly_enabled_connectors
  715          .iter()
  716          .map(|connector_id| AppInvocation {
  717              connector_id: Some(connector_id.clone()),
  718              app_name: connector_names_by_id
  719                  .get(connector_id.as_str())
  720                  .map(|name| (*name).to_string()),
  721              invocation_type: Some(InvocationType::Explicit),
02
L1factconnectors

02 · AGENTS.md 从项目根到 cwd 合并，局部 override 优先且有总预算

源码事实

loader 先定位 project root，再从根到 cwd 搜索 AGENTS.override.md/AGENTS.md；每层只取第一个候选，按顺序拼接并受 project_doc_max_bytes 限制，读取通过环境文件系统抽象。

白话解释

仓库总规矩先读，子目录的局部规矩后读；局部 override 能盖住普通 AGENTS 文件，所有说明不能无限占上下文。

对自研 Harness 的含义

适合 monorepo 分层治理；超预算内容会截断，因此关键安全规则不应只放在大文件末尾。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–24
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 25–49
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

    1  //! AGENTS.md discovery and user instruction assembly.
    2  //!
    3  //! Project-level documentation is primarily stored in files named `AGENTS.md`.
    4  //! Additional fallback filenames can be configured via `project_doc_fallback_filenames`.
    5  //! We include the concatenation of all files found along the path from the
    6  //! project root to the current working directory as follows:
    7  //!
    8  //! 1.  Determine the project root by walking upwards from the current working
    9  //!     directory until a configured `project_root_markers` entry is found.
   10  //!     When `project_root_markers` is unset, the default marker list is used
   11  //!     (`.git`). If no marker is found, only the current working directory is
   12  //!     considered. An empty marker list disables parent traversal.
   13  //! 2.  Collect every `AGENTS.md` found from the project root down to the
   14  //!     current working directory (inclusive) and concatenate their contents in
   15  //!     that order.
   16  //! 3.  We do **not** walk past the project root.

   51  /// Loads project AGENTS.md content and combines it with host-provided user

   53  pub(crate) async fn load_project_instructions(
   54      config: &Config,
   55      user_instructions: Option<UserInstructions>,
   56      environments: &TurnEnvironmentSnapshot,
   57  ) -> Option<LoadedAgentsMd> {
   58      let mut loaded = LoadedAgentsMd::from_user_instructions(user_instructions);
   59      for turn_environment in environments.turn_environments() {
   60          let filesystem = turn_environment.environment.get_filesystem();
   61          match read_agents_md(
   62              config,
   63              filesystem.as_ref(),
   64              &turn_environment.environment_id,
   65              turn_environment.cwd(),
   66          )
   67          .await
   68          {
   69              Ok(Some(docs)) => loaded.entries.extend(docs.entries),
   70              Ok(None) => {}
   71              Err(e) => {
   72                  error!(
   73                      environment_id = turn_environment.environment_id,
   74                      "error trying to find AGENTS.md docs: {e:#}"
   75                  );
   76              }
   77          }
   78      }
   79
   80      (!loaded.is_empty()).then_some(loaded)
   81  }

   88  /// decide how to handle them.

  103          return Ok(None);

  115              Ok(data) => data,

  117              Err(err) => return Err(err),
  118          };
  119          let size = data.len() as u64;
  120          if size > remaining {

L3factconnectors

03 · Skills developer block 不允许被 Harness 转换悄悄丢掉

源码事实

session_skills.rs 明确规定 runtime skills 在 Harness 上层组装成 developer block，每个 Harness 必须映射到最接近的原生格式；解析器支持多行描述、plugin:skill 名称和预算压力下的 rN alias，并有单元测试。

白话解释

换成 Claude/Kimi/Pi 外壳时，用户装的技能仍要跟过去，不能因为消息格式翻译而消失。

对自研 Harness 的含义

跨 Harness 行为更一致；每个 builder 仍需正确调用这些 helper。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–32
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 33–65
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · aaf0aebf8203 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/core/src/harness/session_skills.rs:1–65固定提交 ↗
    1  //! Helpers for surfacing the session's `<skills_instructions>` developer
    2  //! block in emulated harness requests.
    3  //!
    4  //! The workspace harness instruction-role rule says runtime skills are
    5  //! assembled above the harness layer as a `<skills_instructions>` developer
    6  //! block in `prompt.input` and must never be dropped per-harness; each harness
    7  //! maps the block to the closest shape it supports. These helpers locate the
    8  //! block in the prompt input and parse its `### Available skills` entries so
    9  //! harnesses can re-render them in their native skills format.
   10
   11  use codex_protocol::models::ContentItem;
   12  use codex_protocol::models::ResponseItem;
   13  use codex_protocol::protocol::SKILLS_INSTRUCTIONS_CLOSE_TAG;
   14  use codex_protocol::protocol::SKILLS_INSTRUCTIONS_OPEN_TAG;
   15
   16  /// A skill entry parsed from the `### Available skills` list of the session's
   17  /// `<skills_instructions>` developer block. Entries render natively as
   18  /// `- name: description (file: path)`; `path` may be an absolute path or an
   19  /// `rN/...` alias path when the skills list was rendered under budget
   20  /// pressure.
   21  #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
   22  pub(crate) struct SessionSkill {
   23      pub(crate) name: String,
   24      pub(crate) description: String,
   25      pub(crate) path: String,
   26  }
   27
   28  /// Returns the full `<skills_instructions>...</skills_instructions>` text of
   29  /// the session's skills developer block, if the prompt input carries one.
   30  pub(crate) fn find_skills_instructions_text(items: &[ResponseItem]) -> Option<&str> {
   31      items.iter().find_map(|item| {
   32          let ResponseItem::Message { role, content, .. } = item else {
   33              return None;
   34          };
   35          if role != "developer" {
   36              return None;
   37          }
   38          content.iter().find_map(|content_item| {
   39              let ContentItem::InputText { text } = content_item else {
   40                  return None;
   41              };
   42              skills_instructions_body(text).map(|_| text.as_str())
   43          })
   44      })
   45  }
   46
   47  /// Strips the `<skills_instructions>` tags and surrounding newlines, returning
   48  /// the native body (a `## Skills` markdown section).
   49  pub(crate) fn skills_instructions_body(text: &str) -> Option<&str> {
   50      let body = text
   51          .trim()
   52          .strip_prefix(SKILLS_INSTRUCTIONS_OPEN_TAG)?
   53          .strip_suffix(SKILLS_INSTRUCTIONS_CLOSE_TAG)?
   54          .trim_matches('\n');
   55      (!body.is_empty()).then_some(body)
   56  }
   57
   58  /// Parses the session's skills from the `<skills_instructions>` developer
   59  /// block in the prompt input. Returns an empty list when the session has no
   60  /// skills block.
   61  pub(crate) fn parse_session_skills(items: &[ResponseItem]) -> Vec<SessionSkill> {
   62      find_skills_instructions_text(items)
   63          .map(parse_skills_instructions)
   64          .unwrap_or_default()
   65  }
   66
   67  fn parse_skills_instructions(text: &str) -> Vec<SessionSkill> {
   68      let Some(body) = skills_instructions_body(text) else {
   69          return Vec::new();
   70      };
   71      let Some((_, rest)) = body.split_once("### Available skills\n") else {
   72          return Vec::new();
   73      };
   74      let section = rest
   75          .split_once("\n### How to use skills")
   76          .map_or(rest, |(section, _)| section);
   77
   78      // Skill descriptions can contain newlines, so an entry may span multiple
   79      // lines. Every entry ends with its `(file: ...)` suffix, so accumulate
   80      // lines until the entry parses.
   81      let mut skills = Vec::new();
   82      let mut entry = String::new();
   83      for line in section.lines() {
   84          if !entry.is_empty() {
   85              entry.push('\n');
   86          }
   87          entry.push_str(line);
   88          if let Some(skill) = parse_skill_entry(&entry) {
   89              skills.push(skill);
   90              entry.clear();
   91          }
   92      }
   93      skills
   94  }
   95
   96  /// Parses one `- name: description (file: path)` entry. The description may be
   97  /// empty (`- name: (file: path)`) or span multiple lines.
   98  fn parse_skill_entry(entry: &str) -> Option<SessionSkill> {
   99      let rest = entry.trim().strip_prefix("- ")?;
  100      let (head, path) = rest.strip_suffix(')')?.rsplit_once(" (file: ")?;
  101      // Skill names can contain colons (`plugin:skill`) but no spaces, so the
  102      // name/description separator is the first `": "`.
  103      let (name, description) = match head.split_once(": ") {
  104          Some((name, description)) => (name, description),
  105          None => (head.trim_end().strip_suffix(':')?, ""),
  106      };
  107      let name = name.trim();
  108      if name.is_empty() || name.contains(char::is_whitespace) {
  109          return None;
  110      }
  111      Some(SessionSkill {
  112          name: name.to_string(),
  113          description: description.trim().to_string(),
  114          path: path.trim().to_string(),
  115      })
  116  }
  117

  126          )
  127      }

  133          );
  134
  135          assert_eq!(
  136              parse_skills_instructions(&text),

04
L2factconnectors

04 · Hooks 覆盖会话、输入、权限、工具、压缩、停止和子 Agent 生命周期

源码事实

hook event 类型与 runtime 调用点覆盖 SessionStart、UserPromptSubmit、PermissionRequest、PreToolUse/PostToolUse/PostToolUseFailure、PreCompact、Stop、SubagentStart/SubagentStop；hook 可返回附加上下文、权限决定或阻止继续。

白话解释

外部治理系统能在关键关口插卡：开始前补上下文，危险动作前审批，工具后审计，结束前决定是否继续。

对自研 Harness 的含义

适合企业策略和观测；hook 自身是高权限扩展点，配置来源必须受信。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–75
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 76–152
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   14  #[derive(Debug)]
   15  pub enum HookResult {
   16      /// Success: hook completed successfully.
   17      Success,
   18      /// FailedContinue: hook failed, but other subsequent hooks should still execute and the
   19      /// operation should continue.
   20      FailedContinue(Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync + 'static>),
   21      /// FailedAbort: hook failed, other subsequent hooks should not execute, and the operation
   22      /// should be aborted.
   23      FailedAbort(Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync + 'static>),
   24  }
   25
   26  impl HookResult {
   27      pub fn should_abort_operation(&self) -> bool {
   28          matches!(self, Self::FailedAbort(_))
   29      }
   30  }

DIMENSION · SECURITY

安全、审批与权限

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factsecurity

01 · Harness 文件工具先过策略，且同时检查原路径与规范化路径

源码事实

checked_read_path/checked_write_path 解析相对 cwd 后调用 file_system_sandbox_policy；policy_candidates_for_path 同时检查输入路径、canonical path，目标不存在时检查 canonical parent+filename，防止 symlink/别名绕过。

白话解释

即使换成 Claude/Pi 风格的 Read/Write，也不能因为路径里有软链接或奇怪别名绕过沙箱。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼容层与原生工具共享安全边界；搜索 walk 还跳过 symlink、限制 64 层和 5 万项。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 39–65
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   88  pub(crate) fn read_search_file(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
   89      let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(path).ok()?;
   90      let file_type = metadata.file_type();
   91      if !file_type.is_file() || file_type.is_symlink() || metadata.len() > MAX_SEARCH_FILE_BYTES {
   92          return None;
   93      }
   94      fs::read_to_string(path).ok()
   95  }
   96
   97  pub(crate) fn bounded_walk(root: &Path) -> io::Result<Vec<WalkEntry>> {
   98      let root_metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(root)?;
   99      if root_metadata.file_type().is_symlink() {
  100          return Ok(Vec::new());
  101      }
  102      if root_metadata.is_file() {
  103          return Ok(vec![WalkEntry {
  104              path: root.to_path_buf(),
  105              kind: WalkEntryKind::File,
  106          }]);
  107      }
  108      if !root_metadata.is_dir() || is_ignored_dir(root) {
  109          return Ok(Vec::new());
  110      }
  111
  112      let mut entries = Vec::new();
  113      let mut queue = VecDeque::from([(root.to_path_buf(), 0usize)]);
  114      while let Some((dir, depth)) = queue.pop_front() {
  115          if depth >= MAX_WALK_DEPTH || entries.len() >= MAX_WALK_ENTRIES {
  116              continue;
  117          }
  118          for entry in fs::read_dir(&dir)? {
  119              let entry = entry?;
  120              let path = entry.path();
  121              let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(&path)?;
  122              let file_type = metadata.file_type();
  123              if file_type.is_symlink() {
  124                  continue;
  125              }
  126              if file_type.is_file() {
  127                  entries.push(WalkEntry {
  128                      path,
  129                      kind: WalkEntryKind::File,
  130                  });
  131              } else if file_type.is_dir() && !is_ignored_dir(&path) {
  132                  entries.push(WalkEntry {
  133                      path: path.clone(),
  134                      kind: WalkEntryKind::Directory,
  135                  });
  136                  queue.push_back((path, depth + 1));
  137              }
  138              if entries.len() >= MAX_WALK_ENTRIES {
  139                  break;
  140              }
  141          }
  142      }
  143      Ok(entries)
  144  }
  145
  146  pub(crate) fn is_ignored_dir(path: &Path) -> bool {
  147      path.file_name()
  148          .and_then(|name| name.to_str())
  149          .is_some_and(|name| matches!(name, ".git" | "node_modules" | "target" | ".venv"))
  150  }
  151

  162      operation: &str,
  163  ) -> Result<(), FunctionCallError> {
  164      let cwd = primary_cwd(invocation);
  165      let policy = invocation.turn.file_system_sandbox_policy();

02
L1factsecurity

02 · 审批和能力授权是两条轴，OS 沙箱是真实进程变换

源码事实

配置将 approval policy 与 permission profile 分开编译；执行时根据平台把命令变换成 macOS sandbox-exec/Seatbelt、Linux seccomp+bubblewrap/landlock 或 Windows restricted token/private desktop，SandboxType::None 才是不变换。

白话解释

“要不要先问你”与“即使你同意，它最多能碰哪里”是两回事；后者不是提示词，而是操作系统级限制。

对自研 Harness 的含义

这是该分叉继承的最重要企业能力之一；选择 danger/full access 或 SandboxType::None 会主动撤掉这层保护。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 170–214
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 215–260
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · aaf0aebf8203 · 打开账本提交 ↗

  194  pub(crate) fn resolve_permission_profile(
  195      permissions: &PermissionsToml,

  197  ) -> io::Result<PermissionProfileToml> {

  201  }

  203  fn extensible_builtin_parent_profile(profile_name: &str) -> Option<PermissionProfileToml> {
  204      let file_system = match profile_name {
  205          BUILT_IN_READ_ONLY_PROFILE => FileSystemSandboxPolicy::read_only(),
  206          BUILT_IN_WORKSPACE_PROFILE => FileSystemSandboxPolicy::workspace_write(
  207              &[],
  208              /*exclude_tmpdir_env_var*/ false,
  209              /*exclude_slash_tmp*/ false,
  210          ),
  211          _ => return None,
  212      };
  213      Some(permission_profile_toml_from_file_system_policy(file_system))
  214  }
  215
  216  fn permission_profile_toml_from_file_system_policy(
  217      file_system: FileSystemSandboxPolicy,
  218  ) -> PermissionProfileToml {
  219      let mut filesystem = FilesystemPermissionsToml {
  220          glob_scan_max_depth: file_system.glob_scan_max_depth,
  221          entries: BTreeMap::new(),
  222      };
  223      for entry in file_system.entries {
  224          insert_filesystem_permission_toml(&mut filesystem.entries, entry);
  225      }
  226      PermissionProfileToml {
  227          description: None,
  228          extends: None,
  229          workspace_roots: None,
  230          filesystem: Some(filesystem),
  231          network: None,
  232      }
  233  }
  234
  235  fn insert_filesystem_permission_toml(
  236      entries: &mut BTreeMap<String, FilesystemPermissionToml>,
  237      entry: FileSystemSandboxEntry,
  238  ) {
  239      match entry.path {
  240          FileSystemPath::Path { path } => {
  241              entries.insert(
  242                  path.into_path_buf().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
  243                  FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(entry.access),
  244              );
  245          }
  246          FileSystemPath::GlobPattern { pattern } => {
  247              entries.insert(pattern, FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(entry.access));
  248          }
  249          FileSystemPath::Special { value } => {
  250              insert_special_filesystem_permission_toml(entries, value, entry.access);
  251          }
  252      }
  253  }
  254
  255  fn insert_special_filesystem_permission_toml(
  256      entries: &mut BTreeMap<String, FilesystemPermissionToml>,
  257      value: FileSystemSpecialPath,
  258      access: FileSystemAccessMode,
  259  ) {
  260      match value {
  261          FileSystemSpecialPath::Root => {
  262              entries.insert(
  263                  ":root".to_string(),
  264                  FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(access),
  265              );
  266          }
  267          FileSystemSpecialPath::Minimal => {
  268              entries.insert(
  269                  ":minimal".to_string(),
  270                  FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(access),
  271              );
  272          }
  273          FileSystemSpecialPath::ProjectRoots { subpath } => {
  274              insert_scoped_filesystem_permission_toml(
  275                  entries,
  276                  ":workspace_roots".to_string(),
  277                  subpath.unwrap_or_else(|| ".".to_string()),
  278                  access,
  279              );
  280          }
  281          FileSystemSpecialPath::Tmpdir => {
  282              entries.insert(
  283                  ":tmpdir".to_string(),
  284                  FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(access),
  285              );
  286          }
  287          FileSystemSpecialPath::SlashTmp => {
  288              entries.insert(
  289                  ":slash_tmp".to_string(),
  290                  FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(access),
  291              );
  292          }
  293          FileSystemSpecialPath::Unknown { path, subpath } => {
  294              if let Some(subpath) = subpath {
  295                  insert_scoped_filesystem_permission_toml(entries, path, subpath, access);
  296              } else {
  297                  entries.insert(path, FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(access));
  298              }
  299          }
  300      };
  301  }
  302
  303  fn insert_scoped_filesystem_permission_toml(
  304      entries: &mut BTreeMap<String, FilesystemPermissionToml>,
  305      path: String,
  306      subpath: String,
  307      access: FileSystemAccessMode,
  308  ) {
  309      let permission = entries
  310          .entry(path)
  311          .or_insert_with(|| FilesystemPermissionToml::Scoped(BTreeMap::new()));
  312      match permission {
  313          FilesystemPermissionToml::Scoped(scoped_entries) => {
  314              scoped_entries.insert(subpath, access);
  315          }
  316          FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(_) => {
  317              *permission = FilesystemPermissionToml::Scoped(BTreeMap::from([(subpath, access)]));
  318          }
  319      }
  320  }

  327          return Ok(NetworkProxyConfig::default());
  328      }
  329      reject_unknown_builtin_permission_profile(profile_name)?;
  330
  331      let permissions = permissions.ok_or_else(|| {

  237      entry: FileSystemSandboxEntry,
  238  ) {

  240          return;
  241      }
  242

  248              );
  249          }

  251              entries.insert(pattern, FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(entry.access));
  252          }
  253          FileSystemPath::Special { value } => {
  254              insert_special_filesystem_permission_toml(entries, value, entry.access);
  255          }
  256      }
  257  }

08
DIMENSION · OBSERVABILITY

观测、日志与回放

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factobservability

01 · JSONL rollout 是可恢复事件事实源，SQLite/trace 是查询与诊断层

源码事实

会话把 SessionMeta、TurnContext、WorldState、ResponseItem、Compacted、EventMsg 和 inter-agent communication 记录为 RolloutItem；重建器按 turn complete/abort/rollback/compact 复原历史，rollout-trace 再把原始事件归约成会话、工具和推理视图。

白话解释

先保存完整流水账，再从流水账还原聊天、文件、工具和子 Agent 发生了什么。

对自研 Harness 的含义

恢复与审计能力强；事件 schema 演进、敏感字段保留和磁盘生命周期需要治理。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 116–201
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 202–288
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · aaf0aebf8203 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/core/src/session/rollout_reconstruction.rs:116–288固定提交 ↗
  116          rollout_items: &[RolloutItem],
  117      ) -> RolloutReconstruction {
  118          // Replay metadata should already match the shape of the future lazy reverse loader, even
  119          // while history materialization still uses an eager bridge. Scan newest-to-oldest,
  120          // stopping once a surviving replacement-history checkpoint and the required resume metadata
  121          // are both known; then replay only the buffered surviving tail forward to preserve exact
  122          // history semantics.
  123          let has_legacy_compaction_without_window_number =
  124              rollout_items.iter().any(|item| {
  125                  matches!(item, RolloutItem::Compacted(compacted) if compacted.window_number.is_none())
  126              });
  127          let initial_window = if has_legacy_compaction_without_window_number {
  128              None
  129          } else {
  130              rollout_items.iter().find_map(|item| match item {
  131                  RolloutItem::SessionMeta(session_meta) => session_meta
  132                      .meta
  133                      .context_window
  134                      .as_ref()
  135                      .and_then(reconstructed_window_from_session_context_window),
  136                  _ => None,
  137              })
  138          };
  139          let mut base_replacement_history: Option<&[ResponseItem]> = None;
  140          let mut previous_turn_settings = None;
  141          let mut reference_context_item = TurnReferenceContextItem::NeverSet;
  142          let mut world_state_replay = Vec::new();
  143          let mut window = None;
  144          // Rollback is "drop the newest N user turns". While scanning in reverse, that becomes
  145          // "skip the next N user-turn segments we finalize".
  146          let mut pending_rollback_turns = 0usize;
  147          // Borrowed suffix of rollout items newer than the newest surviving replacement-history
  148          // checkpoint. If no such checkpoint exists, this remains the full rollout.
  149          let mut rollout_suffix = rollout_items;
  150          // Reverse replay accumulates rollout items into the newest in-progress turn segment until
  151          // we hit its matching `TurnStarted`, at which point the segment can be finalized.
  152          let mut active_segment: Option<ActiveReplaySegment<'_>> = None;
  153
  154          for (index, item) in rollout_items.iter().enumerate().rev() {
  155              match item {
  156                  RolloutItem::Compacted(compacted) => {
  157                      let active_segment =
  158                          active_segment.get_or_insert_with(ActiveReplaySegment::default);
  159                      active_segment.world_state_replay.push(item);
  160                      if active_segment.window.is_none()
  161                          && let Some(window_number) = compacted.window_number
  162                      {
  163                          active_segment.window = Some(ReconstructedWindow {
  164                              number: window_number,
  165                              first_id: compacted.first_window_id.as_deref().and_then(parse_uuid_v7),
  166                              previous_id: compacted
  167                                  .previous_window_id
  168                                  .as_deref()
  169                                  .and_then(parse_uuid_v7),
  170                              id: compacted.window_id.as_deref().and_then(parse_uuid_v7),
  171                          });
  172                      }
  173                      // Looking backward, compaction clears any older baseline unless a newer
  174                      // `TurnContextItem` in this same segment has already re-established it.
  175                      if matches!(
  176                          active_segment.reference_context_item,
  177                          TurnReferenceContextItem::NeverSet
  178                      ) {
  179                          active_segment.reference_context_item = TurnReferenceContextItem::Cleared;
  180                      }
  181                      if active_segment.base_replacement_history.is_none()
  182                          && let Some(replacement_history) = &compacted.replacement_history
  183                      {
  184                          active_segment.base_replacement_history = Some(replacement_history);
  185                          rollout_suffix = &rollout_items[index + 1..];
  186                      }
  187                  }
  188                  RolloutItem::EventMsg(EventMsg::ThreadRolledBack(rollback)) => {
  189                      pending_rollback_turns = pending_rollback_turns
  190                          .saturating_add(usize::try_from(rollback.num_turns).unwrap_or(usize::MAX));
  191                  }
  192                  RolloutItem::EventMsg(EventMsg::TurnComplete(event)) => {
  193                      let active_segment =
  194                          active_segment.get_or_insert_with(ActiveReplaySegment::default);
  195                      // Reverse replay often sees `TurnComplete` before any turn-scoped metadata.
  196                      // Capture the turn id early so later `TurnContext` / abort items can match it.
  197                      if active_segment.turn_id.is_none() {
  198                          active_segment.turn_id = Some(event.turn_id.clone());
  199                      }
  200                  }
  201                  RolloutItem::EventMsg(EventMsg::TurnAborted(event)) => {
  202                      if let Some(active_segment) = active_segment.as_mut() {
  203                          if active_segment.turn_id.is_none()
  204                              && let Some(turn_id) = &event.turn_id
  205                          {
  206                              active_segment.turn_id = Some(turn_id.clone());
  207                          }
  208                      } else if let Some(turn_id) = &event.turn_id {
  209                          active_segment = Some(ActiveReplaySegment {
  210                              turn_id: Some(turn_id.clone()),
  211                              ..Default::default()
  212                          });
  213                      }
  214                  }
  215                  RolloutItem::EventMsg(EventMsg::UserMessage(_)) => {
  216                      let active_segment =
  217                          active_segment.get_or_insert_with(ActiveReplaySegment::default);
  218                      active_segment.counts_as_user_turn = true;
  219                  }
  220                  RolloutItem::TurnContext(ctx) => {
  221                      let active_segment =
  222                          active_segment.get_or_insert_with(ActiveReplaySegment::default);
  223                      // `TurnContextItem` can attach metadata to an existing segment, but only a
  224                      // real `UserMessage` event should make the segment count as a user turn.
  225                      if active_segment.turn_id.is_none() {
  226                          active_segment.turn_id = ctx.turn_id.clone();
  227                      }
  228                      if turn_ids_are_compatible(
  229                          active_segment.turn_id.as_deref(),
  230                          ctx.turn_id.as_deref(),
  231                      ) {
  232                          active_segment.previous_turn_settings = Some(PreviousTurnSettings {
  233                              model: ctx.model.clone(),
  234                              comp_hash: ctx.comp_hash.clone(),
  235                              realtime_active: ctx.realtime_active,
  236                          });
  237                          if matches!(
  238                              active_segment.reference_context_item,
  239                              TurnReferenceContextItem::NeverSet
  240                          ) {
  241                              active_segment.reference_context_item =
  242                                  TurnReferenceContextItem::Latest(Box::new(ctx.clone()));
  243                          }
  244                      }
  245                  }
  246                  RolloutItem::WorldState(_) => {
  247                      let active_segment =
  248                          active_segment.get_or_insert_with(ActiveReplaySegment::default);
  249                      active_segment.world_state_replay.push(item);
  250                  }
  251                  RolloutItem::EventMsg(EventMsg::TurnStarted(event)) => {
  252                      // `TurnStarted` is the oldest boundary of the active reverse segment.
  253                      if active_segment.as_ref().is_some_and(|active_segment| {
  254                          turn_ids_are_compatible(
  255                              active_segment.turn_id.as_deref(),
  256                              Some(event.turn_id.as_str()),
  257                          )
  258                      }) && let Some(active_segment) = active_segment.take()
  259                      {
  260                          finalize_active_segment(
  261                              active_segment,
  262                              &mut base_replacement_history,
  263                              &mut previous_turn_settings,
  264                              &mut reference_context_item,
  265                              &mut world_state_replay,
  266                              &mut window,
  267                              &mut pending_rollback_turns,
  268                          );
  269                      }
  270                  }
  271                  RolloutItem::ResponseItem(response_item) => {
  272                      let active_segment =
  273                          active_segment.get_or_insert_with(ActiveReplaySegment::default);
  274                      active_segment.counts_as_user_turn |= is_user_turn_boundary(response_item);
  275                  }
  276                  RolloutItem::InterAgentCommunication(_) => {
  277                      let active_segment =
  278                          active_segment.get_or_insert_with(ActiveReplaySegment::default);
  279                      active_segment.counts_as_user_turn = true;
  280                  }
  281                  RolloutItem::EventMsg(_)
  282                  | RolloutItem::SessionMeta(_)
  283                  | RolloutItem::InterAgentCommunicationMetadata { .. } => {}
  284              }
  285
  286              if base_replacement_history.is_some()
  287                  && previous_turn_settings.is_some()
  288                  && !matches!(reference_context_item, TurnReferenceContextItem::NeverSet)
  289              {
  290                  // At this point we have both eager resume metadata values and the replacement-
  291                  // history base for the surviving tail, so older rollout items cannot affect this
  292                  // result.
  293                  break;
  294              }
  295          }
  296
  297          if let Some(active_segment) = active_segment.take() {
  298              finalize_active_segment(
  299                  active_segment,
  300                  &mut base_replacement_history,
  301                  &mut previous_turn_settings,
  302                  &mut reference_context_item,
  303                  &mut world_state_replay,
  304                  &mut window,
  305                  &mut pending_rollback_turns,
  306              );
  307          }
  308
  309          let fallback_window_number = u64::try_from(
  310              rollout_items
  311                  .iter()
  312                  .filter(|item| matches!(item, RolloutItem::Compacted(_)))
  313                  .count(),
  314          )
  315          .unwrap_or(u64::MAX);
  316
  317          let mut history = ContextManager::new();
  318          let mut saw_legacy_compaction_without_replacement_history = false;
  319          if let Some(base_replacement_history) = base_replacement_history {
  320              history.replace(base_replacement_history.to_vec());
  321          }
  322          // Materialize exact history semantics from the replay-derived suffix. The eventual lazy
  323          // design should keep this same replay shape, but drive it from a resumable reverse source
  324          // instead of an eagerly loaded `&[RolloutItem]`.
  325          for item in rollout_suffix {
  326              match item {
  327                  RolloutItem::ResponseItem(response_item) => {
  328                      history.record_items(
  329                          std::iter::once(response_item),
  330                          turn_context.model_info.truncation_policy.into(),
  331                      );
  332                  }
  333                  RolloutItem::InterAgentCommunication(communication) => {
  334                      let response_item = communication.to_model_input_item();
  335                      history.record_items(
  336                          std::iter::once(&response_item),
  337                          turn_context.model_info.truncation_policy.into(),
  338                      );
  339                  }
  340                  RolloutItem::InterAgentCommunicationMetadata { .. } => {}
  341                  RolloutItem::Compacted(compacted) => {
  342                      if let Some(replacement_history) = &compacted.replacement_history {
  343                          // This should actually never happen, because the reverse loop above (to build rollout_suffix)
  344                          // should stop before any compaction that has Some replacement_history
  345                          history.replace(replacement_history.clone());
  346                      } else {
  347                          saw_legacy_compaction_without_replacement_history = true;
  348                          // Legacy rollouts without `replacement_history` should rebuild the
  349                          // historical TurnContext at the correct insertion point from persisted
  350                          // `TurnContextItem`s. These are rare enough that we currently just clear
  351                          // `reference_context_item`, reinject canonical context at the end of the
  352                          // resumed conversation, and accept the temporary out-of-distribution
  353                          // prompt shape.
  354                          // TODO(ccunningham): if we drop support for None replacement_history compaction items,
  355                          // we can get rid of this second loop entirely and just build `history` directly in the first loop.
  356                          let user_messages = compact::collect_user_messages(history.raw_items());
  357                          let rebuilt = compact::build_compacted_history(
  358                              Vec::new(),
  359                              &user_messages,

02
L1riskobservability

02 · Open Interpreter 使用独立分析端点，默认启用但可显式关闭

源码事实

AnalyticsEventsClient 在 analytics_enabled != Some(false) 时创建队列；Open Interpreter 把任何 provider 的事件发送到 oi-new-api.fly.dev 的固定端点，配置 [analytics] enabled=false 可关闭；队列满会丢事件而不阻塞主循环。

白话解释

默认会发产品使用事件到 Open Interpreter 的后端，用户可以在配置里关掉；遥测故障不会卡住编码任务。

对自研 Harness 的含义

企业部署应在基线配置里明确关闭或审查字段，而不是假设换了自建模型就没有遥测。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 54–78
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 79–105
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  168              emitted.clear();

09
DIMENSION · COLLABORATION

协作与子 Agent

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factcollaboration

01 · 多 Agent 是共享控制面的线程树

源码事实

AgentControl 在 root session 创建并共享给所有子 Agent，维护 thread id、parent/child edges、spawn depth、状态与执行 limiter；spawn、message、interrupt、wait、resume 都落到同一 CodexThreadManager。

白话解释

子 Agent 不是主函数里临时递归一下，而是有独立会话和持久关系的线程树。

对自研 Harness 的含义

可以跨步等待、续跑和观测；也需要全局驻留数、运行数和深度限制。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 88–133
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 134–180
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · aaf0aebf8203 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/core/src/agent/control.rs:88–180固定提交 ↗
   88
   89  /// Control-plane handle for multi-agent operations.
   90  /// `AgentControl` is held by each session (via `SessionServices`). It provides capability to
   91  /// spawn new agents and the inter-agent communication layer.
   92  /// An `AgentControl` instance is intended to be created at most once per root thread/session
   93  /// tree. That same `AgentControl` is then shared with every sub-agent spawned from that root,
   94  /// which keeps the registry scoped to that root thread rather than the entire `ThreadManager`.
   95  #[derive(Clone, Default)]
   96  pub(crate) struct AgentControl {
   97      /// ID shared by the whole agent control session. This means every sub-agents from a common
   98      /// root share the same session ID.
   99      session_id: SessionId,
  100      /// Weak handle back to the global thread registry/state.
  101      /// This is `Weak` to avoid reference cycles and shadow persistence of the form
  102      /// `ThreadManagerState -> CodexThread -> Session -> SessionServices -> ThreadManagerState`.
  103      manager: Weak<ThreadManagerState>,
  104      state: Arc<AgentRegistry>,
  105      v2_residency: Arc<V2Residency>,
  106      agent_execution_limiter: Arc<AgentExecutionLimiter>,
  107      /// Session-scoped state shared by the root thread and every cloned sub-agent control handle.
  108      rollout_budget: Arc<RolloutBudget>,
  109  }
  110
  111  impl AgentControl {
  112      /// Construct a new `AgentControl` that can spawn/message agents via the given manager state.
  113      pub(crate) fn new(
  114          manager: Weak<ThreadManagerState>,
  115          rollout_budget: Option<RolloutBudgetConfig>,
  116      ) -> Self {
  117          let control = Self {
  118              manager,
  119              ..Default::default()
  120          };
  121          if let Some(rollout_budget) = rollout_budget {
  122              control.rollout_budget.configure(rollout_budget);
  123          }
  124          control
  125      }
  126
  127      pub(crate) fn with_session_id(mut self, session_id: SessionId, max_threads: usize) -> Self {
  128          self.session_id = session_id;
  129          self.agent_execution_limiter.initialize(max_threads);
  130          self
  131      }
  132
  133      pub(crate) fn session_id(&self) -> SessionId {
  134          self.session_id
  135      }
  136
  137      pub(crate) fn rollout_budget(&self) -> &RolloutBudget {
  138          self.rollout_budget.as_ref()
  139      }
  140
  141      /// Send rich user input items to an existing agent thread.
  142      pub(crate) async fn send_input(
  143          &self,
  144          agent_id: ThreadId,
  145          input: Vec<UserInput>,
  146      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  147          let state = self.upgrade()?;
  148          self.ensure_execution_capacity_for_turn_start(agent_id, /*starts_turn*/ true)
  149              .await?;
  150          self.send_input_after_capacity_check(agent_id, &state, input)
  151              .await
  152      }
  153
  154      async fn send_input_after_capacity_check(
  155          &self,
  156          agent_id: ThreadId,
  157          state: &Arc<ThreadManagerState>,
  158          input: Vec<UserInput>,
  159      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  160          self.handle_thread_request_result(
  161              agent_id,
  162              state,
  163              state.send_op(agent_id, input.into()).await,
  164          )
  165          .await
  166      }
  167
  168      pub(crate) async fn send_inter_agent_communication(
  169          &self,
  170          agent_id: ThreadId,
  171          communication: InterAgentCommunication,
  172          agent_communication_context: AgentCommunicationContext,
  173      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  174          let state = self.upgrade()?;
  175          self.ensure_execution_capacity_for_turn_start(agent_id, communication.trigger_turn)
  176              .await?;
  177          self.send_inter_agent_communication_after_capacity_check(
  178              agent_id,
  179              &state,
  180              communication,
  181              agent_communication_context,
  182          )
  183          .await
  184      }
  185
  186      async fn send_inter_agent_communication_after_capacity_check(
  187          &self,
  188          agent_id: ThreadId,
  189          state: &Arc<ThreadManagerState>,
  190          communication: InterAgentCommunication,
  191          context: AgentCommunicationContext,
  192      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  193          self.submit_inter_agent_communication(agent_id, state, communication, context)
  194              .await
  195      }
  196
  197      async fn submit_inter_agent_communication(
  198          &self,
  199          agent_id: ThreadId,
  200          state: &Arc<ThreadManagerState>,
  201          communication: InterAgentCommunication,
  202          context: AgentCommunicationContext,
  203      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  204          let communication_for_log =
  205              crate::agent_communication::logging_enabled().then(|| communication.clone());
  206          let result = self
  207              .handle_thread_request_result(
  208                  agent_id,
  209                  state,
  210                  state
  211                      .send_op(agent_id, Op::InterAgentCommunication { communication })
  212                      .await,
  213              )
  214              .await;
  215          if let (Some(communication), Ok(communication_id)) =
  216              (communication_for_log, result.as_ref())
  217          {
  218              crate::agent_communication::emit_agent_communication_send(
  219                  communication_id,
  220                  &context,
  221                  &communication,
  222                  agent_id,
  223              );
  224          }
  225          result
  226      }
  227
  228      /// Interrupt the current task for an existing agent thread.
  229      pub(crate) async fn interrupt_agent(&self, agent_id: ThreadId) -> CodexResult<String> {
  230          let state = self.upgrade()?;
  231          self.handle_thread_request_result(
  232              agent_id,
  233              &state,
  234              state.send_op(agent_id, Op::Interrupt).await,
  235          )
  236          .await
  237      }
  238
  239      async fn handle_thread_request_result(
  240          &self,
  241          agent_id: ThreadId,
  242          state: &Arc<ThreadManagerState>,
  243          result: CodexResult<String>,
  244      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  245          if matches!(result, Err(CodexErr::InternalAgentDied)) {
  246              let _ = state.remove_thread(&agent_id).await;
  247              self.forget_v2_residency(agent_id);
  248              self.state.release_spawned_thread(agent_id);
  249          }
  250          result
  251      }

   88  }
   89
   90  /// Control-plane handle for multi-agent operations.
   91  /// `AgentControl` is held by each session (via `SessionServices`). It provides capability to
   92  /// spawn new agents and the inter-agent communication layer.
   93  /// An `AgentControl` instance is intended to be created at most once per root thread/session
   94  /// tree. That same `AgentControl` is then shared with every sub-agent spawned from that root,
   95  /// which keeps the registry scoped to that root thread rather than the entire `ThreadManager`.
   96  #[derive(Clone, Default)]
   97  pub(crate) struct AgentControl {
   98      /// ID shared by the whole agent control session. This means every sub-agents from a common
   99      /// root share the same session ID.
  100      session_id: SessionId,
  101      /// Weak handle back to the global thread registry/state.
  102      /// This is `Weak` to avoid reference cycles and shadow persistence of the form
  103      /// `ThreadManagerState -> CodexThread -> Session -> SessionServices -> ThreadManagerState`.
  104      manager: Weak<ThreadManagerState>,
  105      state: Arc<AgentRegistry>,
  106      v2_residency: Arc<V2Residency>,
  107      agent_execution_limiter: Arc<AgentExecutionLimiter>,
  108      /// Session-scoped state shared by the root thread and every cloned sub-agent control handle.
  109      rollout_budget: Arc<RolloutBudget>,
  110  }
  111
  112  impl AgentControl {
  113      /// Construct a new `AgentControl` that can spawn/message agents via the given manager state.
  114      pub(crate) fn new(
  115          manager: Weak<ThreadManagerState>,
  116          rollout_budget: Option<RolloutBudgetConfig>,
  117      ) -> Self {
  118          let control = Self {
  119              manager,
  120              ..Default::default()
  121          };
  122          if let Some(rollout_budget) = rollout_budget {
  123              control.rollout_budget.configure(rollout_budget);
  124          }
  125          control
  126      }
  127
  128      pub(crate) fn with_session_id(mut self, session_id: SessionId, max_threads: usize) -> Self {
  129          self.session_id = session_id;
  130          self.agent_execution_limiter.initialize(max_threads);
  131          self
  132      }
  133
  134      pub(crate) fn session_id(&self) -> SessionId {
  135          self.session_id
  136      }
  137
  138      pub(crate) fn rollout_budget(&self) -> &RolloutBudget {
  139          self.rollout_budget.as_ref()
  140      }

02
L1factcollaboration

02 · Harness 自己的 Agent/Task 工具复用同一子 Agent 系统

源码事实

Claude/Kimi/ZCode alias 可把 Agent、TaskOutput、TaskStop 等目标工具名映射到内部 multi-agent handler；OpenCode task 也走专用 alias。模型看到的是目标 Harness 语言，后台仍是同一个 AgentControl。

白话解释

外面看像 Claude 的 Agent 工具或 OpenCode 的 task，里面其实都在同一棵线程树上派工。

对自研 Harness 的含义

协作能力不会因换 Harness 丢失；目标 Harness 原生的所有细节并不保证完全复刻。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 616–649
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 650–685
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  687  fn standalone_web_search_enabled(turn_context: &TurnContext) -> bool {
  688      namespace_tools_enabled(turn_context)
  689          && (turn_context.model_info.use_responses_lite
  690              || turn_context
  691                  .config
  692                  .features
  693                  .get()
  694                  .enabled(Feature::StandaloneWebSearch))
  695  }
  696
  697  fn tool_environment_mode(step_context: &StepContext) -> ToolEnvironmentMode {
  698      ToolEnvironmentMode::from_count(step_context.environments.turn_environments().count())
  699  }
  700
  701  #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
  702  fn add_shell_tools(context: &CoreToolPlanContext<'_>, planned_tools: &mut PlannedTools) {
  703      let turn_context = context.step_context.turn.as_ref();
  704      let features = turn_context.config.features.get();
  705      let environment_mode = tool_environment_mode(context.step_context);
  706      if !environment_mode.has_environment() {
  707          return;
  708      }
  709
  710      let allow_login_shell = turn_context.config.permissions.allow_login_shell;
  711      let exec_permission_approvals_enabled = features.enabled(Feature::ExecPermissionApprovals);
  712      let include_environment_id = matches!(environment_mode, ToolEnvironmentMode::Multiple);
  713      let shell_command_options = ShellCommandHandlerOptions {
  714          backend_config: shell_command_backend_for_features(features),
  715          allow_login_shell,
  716          exec_permission_approvals_enabled,
  717      };
  718
  719      match shell_type_for_model_and_features(&turn_context.model_info, features) {
  720          ConfigShellToolType::UnifiedExec => {
  721              planned_tools.add(ExecCommandHandler::new(ExecCommandHandlerOptions {
  722                  allow_login_shell,
  723                  exec_permission_approvals_enabled,
  724                  include_environment_id,
  725                  include_shell_parameter: unified_exec_should_include_shell_parameter(
  726                      turn_context,
  727                      context.step_context,
  728                  ),
  729              }));
  730              planned_tools.add(WriteStdinHandler);
  731
  732              // Keep the legacy shell tool registered while unified exec is
  733              // model-visible.
  734              planned_tools.add_dispatch_only(ShellCommandHandler::new(shell_command_options));
  735          }
  736          ConfigShellToolType::Disabled => {}
  737          ConfigShellToolType::Default
  738          | ConfigShellToolType::Local
  739          | ConfigShellToolType::ShellCommand => {
  740              planned_tools.add(ShellCommandHandler::new(shell_command_options));
  741          }
  742      }
  743  }
  744
  745  fn unified_exec_should_include_shell_parameter(
  746      turn_context: &TurnContext,
  747      step_context: &StepContext,
  748  ) -> bool {
  749      !matches!(
  750          &turn_context.unified_exec_shell_mode,
  751          UnifiedExecShellMode::ZshFork(_)
  752      ) || step_context
  753          .environments
  754          .turn_environments()
  755          .any(|environment| environment.environment.is_remote())
  756  }

11
DIMENSION · RECOVERY

恢复、测试与取舍

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factrecovery

01 · 当前源码不是经典 Python Open Interpreter，而是 Rust/Codex 兼容分叉

源码事实

核心目录、crate、app-server 协议和 Python/TypeScript SDK 仍以 codex/openai-codex 命名；Open Interpreter 通过 Product::OpenInterpreter、interpreter/i 入口、.openinterpreter HOME 和 open-interpreter 包变体建立产品身份。

白话解释

它不是在旧 Python 循环上继续打补丁，而是把一套 Codex 级 Rust Harness 换了产品入口，再加入自己的多 Harness 能力。

对自研 Harness 的含义

评估时必须把它与同批次的 openai/codex 分开：共享基座很多，但 Open Interpreter 的差异主要落在品牌、provider/harness 选择和协议仿真。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 45–61
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 62–79
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   45  /// Product channel information used by branded package variants.
   46  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
   47  pub enum Product {
   48      Codex,
   49      OpenInterpreter,
   50  }
   51
   52  impl Product {
   53      /// A binary shipped inside an Open Interpreter package is Open
   54      /// Interpreter unconditionally — identity must never depend on what the
   55      /// executable happens to be named or aliased to. The argv0 and env-var
   56      /// checks only exist for development builds run straight out of the
   57      /// cargo target directory.
   58      pub fn current() -> Self {
   59          if std::env::var_os(OPEN_INTERPRETER_BRAND_ENV_VAR).is_some()
   60              || is_open_interpreter_argv0()
   61              || is_open_interpreter_install()
   62          {
   63              Self::OpenInterpreter
   64          } else {
   65              Self::Codex
   66          }
   67      }
   68
   69      pub fn display_name(self) -> &'static str {
   70          match self {
   71              Product::Codex => "OpenAI Codex",
   72              Product::OpenInterpreter => "Open Interpreter",
   73          }
   74      }
   75
   76      pub fn command_name(self) -> &'static str {
   77          match self {
   78              Product::Codex => "codex",
   79              Product::OpenInterpreter => "interpreter",
   80          }
   81      }
   82

   84      pub fn codex_compatibility_version(self) -> &'static str {
   85          match self {
   86              Product::Codex => env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"),
   87              Product::OpenInterpreter => OPEN_INTERPRETER_CODEX_COMPATIBILITY_VERSION,
   88          }
   89      }

   94              Product::OpenInterpreter => OPEN_INTERPRETER_RELEASE_NOTES_URL,
   95          }
   96      }

  102          }

  116          }
  117      }
  118

  141  pub fn is_open_interpreter_argv0() -> bool {
  142      std::env::args_os()
  143          .next()
  144          .and_then(|arg0| {
  145              std::path::Path::new(&arg0)
  146                  .file_stem()
  147                  .and_then(|name| name.to_str())
  148                  .map(str::to_owned)
  149          })
  150          .is_some_and(|name| name.starts_with("interpreter") || name == "i")

02
L3factrecovery

02 · 兼容性是被测试的产品契约，不只是目录遗留

源码事实

仓库用 interpreter 二进制替换 CODEX_EXEC_PATH，运行 @openai/codex-sdk 的 thread resume 测试；包布局测试也检查 interpreter 与 i alias 以及 code-mode host。

白话解释

“还能被 Codex SDK 当成 Codex 用”是 CI 级目标，不是偶然能跑。

对自研 Harness 的含义

企业接入可复用 Codex 客户端生态；代价是内部命名和上游演进耦合很深。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 21–27
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 28–36
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L5inferencerecovery

03 · 优势是“一个安全底盘，多种模型原生手感”；主要成本是兼容矩阵爆炸

源码事实

本提交同时维护 15 类 Harness enum、三种 wire protocol、多个独立 request builder、数千行 alias/adapter 与 Harness-aware compaction，并以大量路由/快照/包测试约束行为。

白话解释

它最像“Coding Agent 兼容器”：同一套沙箱和会话底盘，让不同模型吃到熟悉的提示词与工具格式。

对自研 Harness 的含义

建设自有 Agent 时可借鉴“内部统一 IR + 外部 Harness adapter”；但必须为路由、历史翻译、工具参数、压缩和错误恢复建立组合测试，否则适配越多越难证明正确。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 75–152
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 153–231
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

边界 / 风险
  • 这是基于当前源码形态的工程判断，不代表每种 Harness 在目标 benchmark 上一定优于 Native。

codex-rs/core/src/harness/request.rs:75–231固定提交 ↗
   75  pub(crate) fn build_chat_harness_request(
   76      route: ChatHarnessRoute,
   77      turn: ChatHarnessTurn<'_>,
   78  ) -> Result<ChatHarnessRequest, CodexErr> {
   79      let ChatHarnessTurn {
   80          prompt,
   81          harness,
   82          harness_guidance,
   83          model_info,
   84          effort,
   85          thread_id,
   86          session_source,
   87      } = turn;
   88      let guided_prompt = prompt_with_harness_guidance(prompt, harness, harness_guidance);
   89      let yolo_mode = prompt
   90          .base_instructions
   91          .text
   92          .contains("Approval policy is currently never.");
   93      let (request_body, tool_kinds, title_request, postprocess) = match route {
   94          ChatHarnessRoute::DeepSeekTui => {
   95              let (request_body, tool_kinds) = build_deepseek_tui_request(&guided_prompt, model_info)
   96                  .map_err(|err| {
   97                      CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid deepseek-tui request: {err}"))
   98                  })?;
   99              (request_body, tool_kinds, None, ChatHarnessPostprocess::None)
  100          }
  101          ChatHarnessRoute::KimiCli => {
  102              let (request_body, tool_kinds) = build_kimi_cli_request(
  103                  &guided_prompt,
  104                  model_info,
  105                  effort,
  106                  thread_id,
  107                  session_source,
  108                  yolo_mode,
  109              )
  110              .map_err(|err| CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid kimi-cli request: {err}")))?;
  111              (request_body, tool_kinds, None, ChatHarnessPostprocess::None)
  112          }
  113          ChatHarnessRoute::KimiCode => {
  114              let (request_body, tool_kinds) =
  115                  build_kimi_code_request(&guided_prompt, model_info, thread_id).map_err(|err| {
  116                      CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid kimi-code request: {err}"))
  117                  })?;
  118              (request_body, tool_kinds, None, ChatHarnessPostprocess::None)
  119          }
  120          ChatHarnessRoute::LittleCoder => {
  121              let (request_body, tool_kinds) = build_little_coder_request(&guided_prompt, model_info)
  122                  .map_err(|err| {
  123                      CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid little-coder request: {err}"))
  124                  })?;
  125              (request_body, tool_kinds, None, ChatHarnessPostprocess::None)
  126          }
  127          ChatHarnessRoute::MiniSweAgent => {
  128              let (request_body, tool_kinds) =
  129                  build_mini_swe_agent_request(&guided_prompt, model_info).map_err(|err| {
  130                      CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid mini-swe-agent request: {err}"))
  131                  })?;
  132              (
  133                  request_body,
  134                  tool_kinds,
  135                  None,
  136                  ChatHarnessPostprocess::MiniSweAgentNoToolCallFormatError,
  137              )
  138          }
  139          ChatHarnessRoute::Minimal => {
  140              let (request_body, tool_kinds) =
  141                  build_minimal_request(&guided_prompt, model_info, effort).map_err(|err| {
  142                      CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid minimal request: {err}"))
  143                  })?;
  144              (request_body, tool_kinds, None, ChatHarnessPostprocess::None)
  145          }
  146          ChatHarnessRoute::OpenCode => {
  147              let title_request = should_generate_opencode_title(&guided_prompt)
  148                  .then(|| build_opencode_title_request(&guided_prompt, model_info));
  149              let (request_body, tool_kinds) = build_opencode_request(&guided_prompt, model_info)
  150                  .map_err(|err| {
  151                      CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid opencode request: {err}"))
  152                  })?;
  153              (
  154                  request_body,
  155                  tool_kinds,
  156                  title_request,
  157                  ChatHarnessPostprocess::None,
  158              )
  159          }
  160          ChatHarnessRoute::Pi => {
  161              let (request_body, tool_kinds) = build_pi_request(&guided_prompt, model_info)
  162                  .map_err(|err| CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid pi request: {err}")))?;
  163              (request_body, tool_kinds, None, ChatHarnessPostprocess::None)
  164          }
  165          ChatHarnessRoute::QwenCode => {
  166              let (request_body, tool_kinds) =
  167                  build_qwen_code_request(&guided_prompt, model_info, effort, thread_id, yolo_mode)
  168                      .map_err(|err| {
  169                      CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid qwen-code request: {err}"))
  170                  })?;
  171              (request_body, tool_kinds, None, ChatHarnessPostprocess::None)
  172          }
  173          ChatHarnessRoute::SweAgent => {
  174              let (request_body, tool_kinds) = build_swe_agent_request(&guided_prompt, model_info)
  175                  .map_err(|err| {
  176                      CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid swe-agent request: {err}"))
  177                  })?;
  178              (
  179                  request_body,
  180                  tool_kinds,
  181                  None,
  182                  ChatHarnessPostprocess::SweAgentActionCalls {
  183                      has_submit_review: swe_agent_prompt_has_submit_review(&guided_prompt),
  184                  },
  185              )
  186          }
  187          ChatHarnessRoute::Terminus2 => {
  188              let (request_body, tool_kinds, request_kind) =
  189                  build_terminus_2_request(&guided_prompt, model_info).map_err(|err| {
  190                      CodexErr::InvalidRequest(format!("invalid terminus-2 request: {err}"))
  191                  })?;
  192              (
  193                  request_body,
  194                  tool_kinds,
  195                  None,
  196                  ChatHarnessPostprocess::Terminus2ActionCalls {
  197                      request_kind,
  198                      pending_completion: terminus_2_prompt_has_completion_confirmation(
  199                          &guided_prompt,
  200                      ),
  201                  },
  202              )
  203          }
  204      };
  205
  206      Ok(ChatHarnessRequest {
  207          request_body,
  208          tool_kinds,
  209          title_request,
  210          postprocess,
  211      })
  212  }
  213
  214  pub(crate) fn apply_chat_harness_postprocess(
  215      stream: ResponseStream,
  216      postprocess: ChatHarnessPostprocess,
  217  ) -> ResponseStream {
  218      match postprocess {
  219          ChatHarnessPostprocess::None => stream,
  220          ChatHarnessPostprocess::MiniSweAgentNoToolCallFormatError => {
  221              inject_mini_swe_agent_no_tool_call_format_error(stream)
  222          }
  223          ChatHarnessPostprocess::SweAgentActionCalls { has_submit_review } => {
  224              inject_swe_agent_action_calls(stream, has_submit_review)
  225          }
  226          ChatHarnessPostprocess::Terminus2ActionCalls {
  227              request_kind,
  228              pending_completion,
  229          } => inject_terminus_2_action_calls(stream, request_kind, pending_completion),
  230      }
  231  }
  232
  233  pub(crate) fn prompt_with_harness_guidance<'a>(
  234      prompt: &'a Prompt,
  235      harness: &Harness,
  236      enabled: bool,
  237  ) -> std::borrow::Cow<'a, Prompt> {
  238      if !enabled {
  239          return std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(prompt);
  240      }
  241      let Some(guidance) = guidance_for_harness(harness) else {
  242          return std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(prompt);
  243      };
  244      let mut prompt = prompt.clone();
  245      prompt.base_instructions.text = format!("{guidance}\n\n{}", prompt.base_instructions.text);
  246      std::borrow::Cow::Owned(prompt)
  247  }
